Conclusion: TDSC通过联合学习时序一致性结构化表示和稳定亲和矩阵，有效解决了真实世界视频特征违反UoS假设的问题，实现了准确鲁棒的人体运动分割。

Abstract: Human Motion Segmentation (HMS), which aims to partition a video into non-overlapping segments corresponding to different human motions, has recently attracted increasing research attention. Existing HMS approaches are predominantly based on subspace clustering, which are grounded on the assumption that the distribution of high-dimensional temporal features well aligns with a Union-of-Subspaces (UoS). For videos in the real world, however, the raw frame-level features often violate the UoS assumption and yield unsatisfactory segmentation performance. To address this issue, we propose an efficient and effective approach for HMS, named Temporal Deep Self-expressive subspace Clustering (TDSC), which jointly learns temporally consistent structured representations and stabilized affinity for accurate and robust HMS. Specifically, in TDSC, we alternately learn structured representations of the input frame features and self-expressive coefficients via a properly regularized self-expressive model, in which a coding-rate maximization regularizer is incorporated to avoid representation collapse and conform the learned representations to span a desired UoS distribution, and meanwhile, temporal constraints are incorporated to promote temporally adjacent frames to be partitioned into the same groups. Moreover, we develop a temporal momentum averaging mechanism to stabilize affinity evolution and design a reparameterization strategy to enable efficient optimization. We conduct extensive experiments on five benchmark HMS datasets using both conventional (HoG) and up-to-date deep features (i.e., CLIP, DINOv2) to validate the effectiveness of our approach.

</details>

*Fakrul Islam Tushar,Umme Hafsa Momy,Joseph Y. Lo,Geoffrey D. Rubin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出iTRIALSPACE，一个可编程的评估框架，通过四阶段流水线（结节档案、试验规范、解剖感知掩膜插入、ControlNet条件合成）构建可控虚拟病变试验，实现对肺CT模型的受控评估。在55,469样本的虚拟病变研究中，合成数据与真实数据FID相当，性能排名与真实临床数据高度相关（ρ=0.93），并揭示了固定分布基准无法发现的模型行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有肺部CT模型评估依赖静态回顾性基准数据集，这些数据将病变大小、肺叶分布、解剖结构和采集上下文等变量混杂在一起，难以确定什么因素真正驱动模型准确性。缺乏能够分离和独立控制这些因素的评估框架。

Method: iTRIALSPACE包含四阶段流水线：1）多数据集结节档案（7个公开CT来源，13,140个标注结节，54维属性）；2）显式试验规范定义；3）解剖感知掩膜插入；4）ControlNet条件CT合成。支持13种试验模式，可独立控制病变大小、位置、宿主特征等变量。

Result: 在55,469样本的虚拟病变研究中，合成数据与真实数据FID相当，性能排名与真实临床数据高度相关（ρ=0.93, p<10^-15）。暴露了固定基准无法发现的问题：肺叶均衡采样下模型尺寸预测能力崩溃，双交叉分析中宿主-供体方差比分别达8.9x和3.3x。

Conclusion: iTRIALSPACE提供了一种可审计的评估基础设施，能够进行可控、可证伪的模型测试，超越了传统静态回顾性基准的局限。

Abstract: We introduce iTRIALSPACE, a programmable evaluation framework for controlled assessment of lung CT models. Standard benchmarks are static retrospective collections that entangle lesion size, lobe prevalence, anatomy, and acquisition context, making it difficult to determine what structurally drives model accuracy. iTRIALSPACE addresses this limitation by composing real clinical CTs and lesion profiles into controlled virtual lesion trials through a four-stage pipeline: multidataset nodule profiling, explicit trial specification, anatomy-aware mask insertion, and ControlNet-conditioned CT synthesis. The framework is built on a unified 54-attribute nodule-profile dataset spanning 13,140 annotated nodules from seven public CT sources and instantiated as 13 trial modes. We evaluate iTRIALSPACE in a 55,469-sample Virtual Lesion Study spanning three medical VLMs, four spatialguidance conditions, and three clinical tasks. Across all 13 modes, the synthetic substrate remains within the real-to-real FID baseline, and synthetic performance rankings transfer strongly to real clinical data ($ρ$ = 0.93, p < 10$^{-15}$). Controlled trial modes expose findings unavailable to fixed-distribution benchmarks, including shortcut-driven size prediction collapse under lobe-equalized sampling and hostto-donor variance ratios of 8.9x and 3.3x in twin-cross analysis. These results position iTRIALSPACE as an auditable evaluation infrastructure for controlled, falsifiable testing beyond static retrospective benchmarks.

</details>


### [39] [X-OmniClaw Technical Report: A Unified Mobile Agent for Multimodal Understanding and Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05765)
*Xiaoming Ren,Ru Zhen,Chao Li,Yang Song,Qiuxia Hou,Yanhao Zhang,Peng Liu,Qi Qi,Quanlong Zheng,Qi Wu,Zhenyi Liao,Binqiang Pan,Haobo Ji,Haonan Lu*

TL;DR: X-OmniClaw是一个面向Android生态的统一移动端智能体，通过集成感知、记忆与动作的单一架构，实现多模态理解与交互。它包含Omni Perception（统一多模态输入管道）、Omni Memory（结合运行时工作记忆与长期个性化记忆）和Omni Action（混合定位策略+行为克隆/轨迹回放），在多样化场景中提升了交互效率与任务可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受OpenClaw启发，移动端个人代理需要处理复杂且直观的交互，现有方案缺乏统一的多模态感知、记忆与行动架构，难以实现高上下文感知的个性化移动任务执行。

Method: 提出X-OmniClaw统一架构，包含三个核心模块：1）Omni Perception：统一多模态输入管道，整合UI状态、视觉上下文和语音输入，通过时序对齐模块将原始数据转化为结构化多模态意图表示；2）Omni Memory：结合运行时工作记忆（任务连续性）与从本地数据蒸馏的长期个人记忆，实现个性化增强；3）Omni Action：采用混合定位策略（结构XML元数据+视觉感知），并通过行为克隆与轨迹回放将用户导航捕捉为可复用技能，实现精确的直接访问执行。

Result: 在多样化场景的演示中，X-OmniClaw有效提升了交互效率与任务可靠性，为下一代移动端原生个人助手提供了实用的架构蓝图。

Conclusion: X-OmniClaw通过统一的感知-记忆-行动架构，展示了移动端智能体处理复杂任务的能力，其模块化设计为未来移动端个人助手的发展提供了可参考的技术路径。

Abstract: Inspired by the development of OpenClaw, there is a growing demand for mobile-based personal agents capable of handling complex and intuitive interactions. In this technical report, we introduce X-OmniClaw, a unified mobile agent designed for multimodal understanding and interaction in the Android ecosystem. This unified architecture of perception, memory, and action enables the agent to handle complex mobile tasks with high contextual awareness. Specifically, Omni Perception provides a unified multimodal ingress pipeline that integrates UI states, real-world visual contexts, and speech inputs, leveraging a temporal alignment module to decompose raw data into structured multimodal intent representations. Omni Memory leverages multimodal memory optimization to enhance personalized intelligence by integrating runtime working memory for task continuity with long-term personal memory distilled from local data, enabling highly context-aware and personalized interactions. Finally, Omni Action employs a hybrid grounding strategy that combines structural XML metadata with visual perception for robust interaction. Through Behavior Cloning and Trajectory Replay, the system captures user navigation as reusable skills, enabling precise direct-access execution. Demonstrations across diverse scenarios show that X-OmniClaw effectively enhances interaction efficiency and task reliability, providing a practical architectural blueprint for the next generation of mobile-native personal assistants.

</details>


### [40] [The autoPET3 Challenge -- Automated Lesion Segmentation in Whole-Body PET/CT - Multitracer Multicenter Generalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05775)
*Jakob Dexl,Katharina Jeblick,Andreas Mittermeier,Balthasar Schachtner,Anna Theresa Stüber,Johanna Topalis,Maximilian Rokuss,Fabian Isensee,Klaus H. Maier-Hein,Hamza Kalisch,Jens Kleesiek,Constantin M. Seibold,Hussain Alasmawi,Lap Yan Lennon Chan,Yixuan Yuan,Alexander Jaus,Rainer Stiefelhagen,Pauline Ornela Megne Choudja,Konstantin Nikolaou,Christian La Fougère,Sergios Gatidis,Matthias P. Fabritius,Maurice Heimer,Gizem Abaci,Lalith Kumar Shiyam Sundar,Rudolf A. Werner,Jens Ricke,Clemens C. Cyran,Thomas Küstner,Michael Ingrisch*

TL;DR: 本文报道了第三届autoPET挑战赛（MICCAI 2024），该挑战赛在组合泛化设定下基准测试了全身PET/CT中的自动病灶分割。训练数据包括1,014例[18F]-FDG PET/CT和597例[18F]/[68Ga]-PSMA PET/CT，是迄今最大的公开标注PSMA PET/CT数据集。测试集涵盖四种示踪剂-中心组合，其中两种为未见过的组合。17支队伍提交了27种算法，多为基于nnU-Net的3D网络。最优算法平均DSC为0.66，相比基线DSC提升8%，假阴性体积减少5 mL。主要结论：(1) 域内多示踪剂PET/CT分割已接近读者一致性；(2) 组合泛化到未见示踪剂-中心组合仍是开放问题，主要由系统性体积高估导致；(3) 异质性和病例难度对性能变化的影响远大于顶级队伍间的算法选择。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的PET/CT病灶分割方法往往在特定示踪剂和中心数据上训练和测试，缺乏对组合泛化（即训练时未见的示踪剂-中心组合）能力的系统评估。本文旨在填补这一空白，通过组织第三届autoPET挑战赛，建立一个标准化的基准来评估真实临床场景下的模型泛化能力。

Method: 组织第三届autoPET挑战赛，训练集包含1,014例FDG（蒂宾根大学医院）和597例PSMA（LMU慕尼黑大学医院）PET/CT数据。测试集200例涵盖四种示踪剂-中心组合，其中两种为训练时未见的组合。设置数据驱动奖项，限制参与者使用固定基线模型以隔离数据策略的影响。17支队伍提交27种算法，主要为nnU-Net-based 3D网络，采用PET/CT通道拼接。

Result: 最优算法在四种测试条件下平均DSC 0.66、FNV 3.18 mL、FPV 2.78 mL，相比基线DSC提升8%，FNV减少5 mL。排名在bootstrap重采样和替代排名方案下保持稳定。提供了患者级和病灶级深层次分析。

Conclusion: (1) 域内多示踪剂PET/CT分割已足够好，接近读者间一致性；(2) 组合泛化到未见过的示踪剂-中心组合仍是开放问题，主要源于系统性体积高估；(3) 病例异质性和难度对性能差异的解释力远大于顶级算法之间的选择。

Abstract: We report the design and results of the third autoPET challenge (MICCAI 2024), which benchmarked automated lesion segmentation in whole-body PET/CT under a compositional generalization setting. Training data comprised 1,014 [18F]-FDG PET/CT studies from the University Hospital Tübingen and 597 [18F]/[68Ga]-PSMA PET/CT studies from the LMU University Hospital Munich, constituting the largest publicly available annotated PSMA PET/CT dataset to date. The held-out test set of 200 studies covered four tracer-center combinations, two of which represented unseen compositional pairings. A complementary data-centric award category isolated the contribution of data handling strategies by restricting participants to a fixed baseline model. Seventeen teams submitted 27 algorithms, predominantly nnU-Net-based 3D networks with PET/CT channel concatenation. The top-ranked algorithm achieved a mean DSC of 0.66, FNV of 3.18 mL, and FPV of 2.78 mL across all four test conditions, improving DSC by 8% and reducing the false-negative volume by 5 mL relative to the provided baseline. Ranking was stable across bootstrap resampling and alternative ranking schemes for the top tier. Beyond the benchmark, we provide an in-depth analysis of segmentation performance at the patient and lesion level. Three main conclusions can be drawn: (1) in-domain multitracer PET/CT segmentation is sufficient and probably approaching reader agreement; (2) compositional generalization to unseen tracer-center combinations remains an open problem mainly driven by systematic volume overestimation; (3) heterogeneity and case difficulty drive performance variation substantially more than the choice of algorithm among top-ranked teams.

</details>


### [41] [Steering Visual Generation in Unified Multimodal Models with Understanding Supervision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05781)
*Zeyu Liu,Zanlin Ni,Yang Yue,Cheng Da,Huan Yang,Di Zhang,Kun Gai,Gao Huang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出UNO框架，通过面向理解的后训练，将理解任务作为生成模型的直接监督信号，实现理解与生成的协同增强。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有统一多模态模型将理解与生成模块解耦，虽在各任务上表现优异，但削弱了二者间的相互增强潜力，导致协同效果不确定。

Method: 提出UNO（Understanding-Oriented Post-Training）轻量级框架，通过引入语义抽象（字幕生成）和结构细节（视觉回归）目标，使理解信号能够有效梯度回传至生成表征。

Result: 在图像生成和编辑任务上的大量实验表明，理解可以作为生成的有效催化剂，提升生成性能。

Conclusion: 理解任务不仅可独立存在，还能作为直接监督信号引导生成表征，恢复理解与生成之间的协同关系。

Abstract: Unified multimodal models are envisioned to bridge the gap between understanding and generation. Yet, to achieve competitive performance, state-of-the-art models adopt largely decoupled understanding and generation components. This design, while effective for individual tasks, weakens the connection required for mutual enhancement, leaving the potential synergy empirically uncertain. We propose to explicitly restore this synergy by introducing Understanding-Oriented Post-Training (UNO), a lightweight framework that treats understanding not only as a distinct task, but also a direct supervisory signal to steer generative representations. By incorporating objectives that encode semantic abstraction (captioning) and structural details (visual regression), we enable effective gradient flow from understanding to generation. Extensive experiments on image generation and editing demonstrate that understanding can serve as an effective catalyst for generation.

</details>


### [42] [Na-IRSTD: Enhancing Infrared Small Target Detection via Native-Resolution Feature Selection and Fusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05804)
*Qian Xu,Chi Zhang,Qiming Zhang,Xi Li,Haojuan Yuan,Mingjin Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了Na-IRSTD，一种原生分辨率特征提取与融合框架，通过保留细节信息、结合令牌缩减与选择策略，在四个基准数据集上实现了红外小目标检测的最先进性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有红外小目标检测方法在特征下采样过程中丢失了小目标的细节信息，导致定位精度受限。作者旨在解决分辨率瓶颈，保留原生分辨率特征以提升小目标检测能力。

Method: 1) 提出原生分辨率特征提取与融合框架（Na-IRSTD），保留小目标的细微线索；2) 引入令牌缩减与选择策略，高效筛选目标补丁，增强低层细节特征，同时降低计算负担。

Result: 在四个公开数据集上达到最先进性能；令牌缩减与选择策略在多个数据集上展现出鲁棒性和有效性。

Conclusion: 通过原生分辨率特征保留和高效的令牌处理，Na-IRSTD有效克服了传统方法的分辨率限制，在实现高性能的同时控制了计算开销，为红外小目标检测提供了新范式。

Abstract: Infrared small target detection (IRSTD) faces the inherent challenge of precisely localizing dim targets amid complex background clutter. While progress has been made, existing methods usually follow conventional strategies to downsample features and discard small targets' details, resulting in suboptimal performance. In this paper, we present Na-IRSTD, a native-resolution feature extraction and fusion framework for IRSTD. This framework elegantly incorporates native-resolution features to preserve subtle target cues, overcoming the resolution limitations of existing infrared approaches and significantly improving the model's ability to localize small targets. We also introduce an effective token reduction and selection strategy, which selects target patches with high accuracy and confidence, boosting the low-level details of the feature while effectively reducing native-resolution patch tokens compared to dense processing, thereby avoiding imposing an unbearable computational burden. Extensive experiments demonstrate the robustness and effectiveness of our token reduction and selection strategy across multiple public datasets. Ultimately, our Na-IRSTD model achieves state-of-the-art performance on four benchmarks.

</details>


### [43] [CXR-ContraBench: Benchmarking Negated-Option Attraction in Medical VLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05810)
*Zhengru Fang,Yanan Ma,Yu Guo,Senkang Hu,Yixian Zhang,Hangcheng Cao,Wenbo Ding,Yuguang Fang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文发现医学视觉语言模型在胸部X光片问答中存在"否定选项吸引"现象——即使影像有明显病变，模型仍倾向选择"无病变"选项。作者构建了CXR-ContraBench基准，揭示该问题在多个模型上持续存在（MedGemma准确率仅31.49%），并提出无需重训练的QCCV-Neg方法将准确率提升至96.60%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医学视觉语言模型在回答胸部X光片问题时，当影像显示"实变"但问题询问哪种征象存在时，模型可能回答"无实变"。这种极性反转输出的临床陈述与影像证据矛盾，是比单纯错误更严重的安全隐患。作者希望系统研究这一失败模式。

Method: 1) 构建CXR-ContraBench基准，包含内部ReXVQA和外部OpenI、CheXpert协议，聚焦"存在性"问题和"缺失性"问题；2) 测试MedGemma和Qwen2.5-VL在直接存在性探针上的表现；3) 评估思维链提示的影响；4) 提出QCCV-Neg（问题条件一致性验证器），无需重训练即可修复极性混淆子集。

Result: MedGemma和Qwen2.5-VL在直接存在性探针上准确率分别仅为31.49%和30.21%；在CheXpert训练集上，两者在超过62%的存在性问题中选择否定选项。思维链提示能减少部分存在性反转但会放大缺失性矛盾。QCCV-Neg将两者的准确率分别提升至96.60%和95.32%。

Conclusion: 标准准确率指标会掩盖临床上重要的推理时极性反转失败。本文揭示的"否定选项吸引"现象是医学VLMs的系统性问题，QCCV-Neg提供了一种无需重训练的有效修复方案。

Abstract: When a chest X-ray shows consolidation but the question asks which finding is present, a medical vision-language model may answer "No consolidation." This is more than an incorrect choice: it is a polarity reversal that emits a clinical statement contradicting the image. We study this failure as negated-option attraction, where a model is drawn to a negated answer option even when it conflicts with both the visual evidence and the question. We introduce CXR-ContraBench (Chest X-Ray Contradiction Benchmark), a diagnostic benchmark spanning internal ReXVQA slices and external OpenI and CheXpert protocols. The benchmark centers on present-finding questions, where selecting "No X" despite visible X creates the main clinical risk, and uses absent-finding questions as secondary tests of whether models copy negated wording. Across CheXpert protocols, the failure is substantial and persistent. On a strict direct presence probe, MedGemma and Qwen2.5-VL reach only 31.49% and 30.21% accuracy, respectively; on a matched 135,754-record CheXpert training-split protocol, both models select negated options on over 62% of presence questions. Chain-of-thought prompting reduces some presence-side reversals but does not eliminate them and can amplify absence-side contradictions. Finally, QCCV-Neg (Question-Conditioned Consistency Verifier for Negation) deterministically repairs the measured polarity-confused subset without retraining, raising MedGemma and Qwen2.5-VL to 96.60% and 95.32% accuracy on the direct presence probe. These results show that standard accuracy can hide a clinically meaningful inference-time polarity failure. Source code and benchmark construction scripts are available at https://github.com/fangzr/cxr-contrabench-code.

</details>


### [44] [ChartZero: Synthetic Priors Enable Zero Shot Chart Data Extraction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05820)
*Md Touhidul Islam,Yasir Mahmud,Sujan Kumar Saha,Mark Tehranipoor,Farimah Farahmandi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ChartZero提出了一种基于合成先验的零样本图表数据提取框架，仅用简单数学函数生成的合成数据训练，通过全局正交实例损失解决曲线断裂问题，并利用VLM引导的图例匹配替代刚性空间规则，在无真实标注的情况下显著提升泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图表数据提取方法面临三大瓶颈：1）真实世界标注数据极度稀缺且成本高昂；2）细薄交叉曲线易断裂、细节丢失；3）图例与数据点的语义关联依赖脆弱的空间启发式规则，无法适应任意布局。此外，现有评估协议只评测子任务而非端到端重建，难以衡量真实进展。

Method: 1）纯合成数据训练：仅使用简单数学函数生成合成线图，完全绕过真实标注瓶颈；2）全局正交实例损失（GOI loss）：解决曲线碎片化问题，保持细薄交叉曲线的结构完整性；3）VLM引导的图例匹配策略：利用开放词汇的视觉-语言模型替代刚性空间规则，适应任意图例位置。

Result: 在专门设计的端到端重建基准上，ChartZero显著提升了零样本场景下的图表数字化能力，无需任何真实世界监督即可达到领先性能。

Conclusion: ChartZero证明了合成先验在零样本图表数据提取中的有效性，为突破真实标注瓶颈提供了可行路径，并推动了图表数字化领域评估标准向端到端重建范式的转变。

Abstract: Automated data extraction from line charts remains fundamentally bottlenecked by extreme stylistic diversity and a severe scarcity of comprehensively annotated, real-world datasets. Current end-to-end pipelines depend heavily on costly manual annotations, crippling their ability to generalize across arbitrary aesthetics and grid layouts. Furthermore, existing models suffer from two critical failure modes during reconstruction. First, extracting thin, intersecting curves frequently causes structural fragmentation and the erasure of fine visual details, as standard architectures struggle against complex backgrounds. Second, semantic association is notoriously error-prone; current pipelines rely on rigid spatial heuristics that easily break down against the unpredictable legend placements of in-the-wild charts. Finally, measuring true progress is hindered by evaluation protocols that assess isolated sub-tasks rather than holistic, end-to-end data reconstruction. To address these foundational issues, we introduce ChartZero, a parsing framework that leverages synthetic priors to enable robust zero-shot chart data extraction. By training exclusively on a purely synthetic dataset of simple mathematical functions, our model completely bypasses the real-world annotation bottleneck. We overcome curve fragmentation via a novel Global Orthogonal Instance (GOI) loss, and replace brittle spatial rules with an open-vocabulary, Vision-Language Model (VLM)-guided legend matching strategy. Accompanied by a new metric and benchmark specifically designed for full end-to-end reconstruction, our evaluations demonstrate that ChartZero significantly advances generalized plot digitization without requiring real-world supervision. Code and dataset will be released upon acceptance.

</details>


### [45] [Unifying Scientific Communication: Fine-Grained Correspondence Across Scientific Media](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05831)
*Megha Mariam K. M,Vineeth N. Balasubramanian,C. V. Jawahar*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出多模态会议数据集（MCD），连接论文、幻灯片、演讲视频和讲解视频，评估多种模型在跨格式细粒度对应发现上的能力，发现视觉-语言模型鲁棒但细粒度对齐不足，嵌入模型擅长文本-视觉对应但公式和符号内容形成独立聚类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科研传播日益多模态化（文本、图像、语音），但不同格式材料间缺乏结构化链接，难以追溯概念、视觉和解释之间的对应关系，限制了统一探索和分析。

Method: 构建MCD数据集，集成同一研究的论文、幻灯片、演讲视频和讲解视频；评估嵌入模型和视觉-语言模型在细粒度跨格式对应发现上的表现。

Result: 视觉-语言模型整体鲁棒但细粒度对齐表现不佳；嵌入模型在文本-视觉对应上表现良好，但方程和符号内容在嵌入空间中形成独立聚类。

Conclusion: 揭示了当前方法在跨模态科研内容理解上的优势与局限，为未来研究指明方向，并开源数据集以促进可复现性。

Abstract: The communication of scientific knowledge has become increasingly multimodal, spanning text, visuals, and speech through materials such as research papers, slides, and recorded presentations. These different representations collectively convey a study's reasoning, results, and insights, offering complementary perspectives that enrich understanding. However, despite their shared purpose, such materials are rarely connected in a structured way. The absence of explicit links across formats makes it difficult to trace how concepts, visuals, and explanations correspond, limiting unified exploration and analysis of research content. To address this gap, we introduce the Multimodal Conference Dataset (MCD), the first benchmark that integrates research papers, presentation videos, explanatory videos, and slides from the same works. We evaluate a range of embedding-based and vision-language models to assess their ability to discover fine-grained cross-format correspondences, establishing the first systematic benchmark for this task. Our results show that vision-language models are robust but struggle with fine-grained alignment, while embedding-based models capture text-visual correspondences well but equations and symbolic content form distinct clusters in the embedding space. These findings highlight both the strengths and limitations of current approaches and point to key directions for future research in multimodal scientific understanding. To ensure reproducibility, we release the resources for MCD at https://github.com/meghamariamkm2002/MCD

</details>


### [46] [VideoRouter: Query-Adaptive Dual Routing for Efficient Long-Video Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05848)
*Kuanwei Lin,Wenhao Zhang,Ge Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: VideoRouter提出了一种查询自适应的双路由框架，通过语义路由和图像路由实现预算化的视觉证据分配，在长视频理解中可减少高达67.9%的token数量，同时提升或保持性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长视频推理中视觉token序列过长导致内存和延迟瓶颈，现有压缩方法要么弱查询感知，要么对所有帧使用固定压缩策略，无法适应视觉证据在时间上不均匀分布的情况。

Method: 提出VideoRouter双路由框架：1）语义路由预测全局分配策略（宽时间覆盖 vs 自适应高分辨率保留）；2）图像路由利用早期LLM层评分帧相关性，实现低相关帧激进压缩、关键帧保留细节。并构建了两个监督数据集Video-QTR-10K和Video-FLR-200K。

Result: 在VideoMME、MLVU和LongVideoBench上，VideoRouter在相同或更低预算下持续优于InternVL基线，最高实现67.9%的token压缩率。

Conclusion: 查询感知的自适应压缩策略比固定策略更有效，双路由框架能根据查询需求动态分配预算，显著提升长视频推理的效率与性能。

Abstract: Video large multimodal models increasingly face a scalability bottleneck: long videos produce excessively long visual-token sequences, which sharply increase memory and latency during inference. While existing compression methods are effective in specific settings, most are either weakly query-aware or apply a fixed compression policy across frames, proving suboptimal when visual evidence is unevenly distributed over time. To address this, we present VideoRouter, a query-adaptive dual-router framework built on InternVL for budgeted evidence allocation. The Semantic Router predicts the dominant allocation policy, choosing between broad temporal coverage and adaptive high-resolution preservation, while the Image Router uses early LLM layers to score frame relevance. This enables aggressive compression on less relevant frames while preserving detail on critical evidence frames. To train both routers, we build Video-QTR-10K for allocation-policy supervision and Video-FLR-200K for frame-relevance supervision. Experiments on VideoMME, MLVU, and LongVideoBench show that VideoRouter consistently improves over the InternVL baseline under comparable or lower budgets, achieving up to a 67.9% token reduction.

</details>


### [47] [Align3D-AD: Cross-Modal Feature Alignment and Dual-Prompt Learning for Zero-shot 3D Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05850)
*Letian Bai,Xuanming Cao,Juan Du,Chengyu Tao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Align3D-AD，一种利用辅助类别RGB模态作为跨模态指导的两阶段零样本3D异常检测框架，通过跨模态特征对齐和模态感知提示学习，在多数据集上超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有零样本3D异常检测方法主要将3D观测投影为多视图表示，但这些表示主要捕获几何线索而非真实视觉语义，且使用预训练于RGB数据的视觉编码器处理，导致编码器与投影表示之间存在显著域差距。

Method: 提出Align3D-AD两阶段框架：(1) 跨模态特征对齐范式，将渲染特征映射到RGB语义空间，并引入语义一致性重加权策略细化对齐；(2) 模态感知提示学习框架，为RGB对齐特征和渲染特征分配独立提示，通过双提示对比对齐增强判别能力。

Result: 在MVTec3D-AD、Eyecandies和Real3D-AD数据集上，在one-vs-rest和跨数据集设置下均一致优于现有零样本方法，展现出良好的泛化能力和鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 通过RGB模态的跨模态指导有效解决了域差距问题，验证了该方法在零样本3D异常检测中的有效性和泛化能力。

Abstract: Zero-shot 3D anomaly detection aims to identify anomalies without access to training data from target categories. However, existing methods mainly rely on projecting 3D observations into multi-view representations that primarily capture geometric cues rather than realistic visual semantics and process them with vision encoders pretrained on RGB data, leading to a significant domain gap between the encoder and the projected representations. To address this issue, we propose Align3D-AD, a unified two-stage framework that leverages the RGB modality from auxiliary categories as cross-modal guidance for zero-shot 3D anomaly detection. First, we introduce a cross-modal feature alignment paradigm that maps rendering features into the RGB semantic space. Unlike prior works that implicitly rely on pretrained encoders, our method enables direct semantic transfer from RGB observations. A semantic consistency reweighting strategy is further introduced to refine feature alignment by reweighting local regions according to holistic semantic consistency. Second, we propose a modality-aware prompt learning framework with dual-prompt contrastive alignment. By assigning independent prompts to RGB-aligned and rendering features, our method captures complementary semantics across modalities, while the contrastive alignment further enhances prompt representations to improve discriminability. Extensive experiments on MVTec3D-AD, Eyecandies, and Real3D-AD demonstrate that Align3D-AD consistently outperforms existing zero-shot methods under both one-vs-rest and cross-dataset settings, highlighting its generalization capability and robustness. Code and the dataset will be made available once our paper is accepted.

</details>


### [48] [InkDiffuser: High-Fidelity One-shot Chinese Calligraphy via Differentiable Morphological Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05865)
*Kunchong Shi,Jing Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出InkDiffuser，一种基于扩散模型的一次性中国书法生成框架，通过高频增强机制和可微分墨水结构损失，从单张参考字形生成具有真实墨水渲染效果的高质量书法字体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有书法生成方法存在笔画渲染质量差、墨迹形态不真实的问题，输出的视觉保真度和艺术流畅性有限。

Method: 提出InkDiffuser扩散生成框架，包含两个核心贡献：1）高频增强机制，通过显式融合高频表征来提取更精确的字体结构信息；2）可微分墨水结构损失（DIS），将可微分形态学操作集成到扩散过程中，实现笔迹结构的显式分解与笔画轮廓的精细优化。

Result: 在多种书体风格和复杂字符上的实验表明，InkDiffuser仅需单个参考字形即可生成具有真实墨水渲染效果的优质书法字体，在结构一致性、细节保真度和视觉真实性方面均优于现有的少样本字体生成方法。

Conclusion: InkDiffuser通过高频增强和可微分墨水结构损失有效解决了书法生成中笔画渲染和墨迹形态不真实的问题，实现了高保真的一次性书法字体合成。

Abstract: Current Chinese calligraphy generation methods suffer from poor stroke rendering and unrealistic ink morphology, resulting in outputs with limited visual fidelity and artistic fluidity. To address this problem, we propose \textbf{InkDiffuser}, a diffusion-based generative framework for one-shot Chinese calligraphy synthesis. To guarantee high-fidelity rendering, we introduce two core contributions: a high-frequency enhancement mechanism and a Differentiable Ink Structure (DIS) loss that explicitly regularizes ink morphology. Inspired by the observation that high-frequency information in individual samples typically carries contour details, we enhance content extraction by explicitly fusing high-frequency representations for more accurate font structure. Furthermore, we propose a differentiable ink structure loss that integrates differentiable morphological operations into the diffusion process. By allowing the model to learn an explicit decomposition of ink-trace structures, DIS facilitates fine-grained refinement of stroke contours and delivers significantly improved visual realism in the generated calligraphy. Extensive experiments on various calligraphic styles and complex characters demonstrate that InkDiffuser can generate superior calligraphy fonts with realistic ink rendering effects from only a single reference glyph and outperform existing few-shot font generation approaches in structural consistency, detail fidelity, and visual authenticity. The code is available at the following address: https://github.com/JingVIPLab/InkDiffuser.

</details>


### [49] [Training-Free Dense Hand Contact Estimation with Multi-Modal Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05886)
*Daniel Sungho Jung,Kyoung Mu Lee*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出ContactPrompt，一种无需训练、零样本的密集手部接触估计方法，利用多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）的推理能力，通过手部分割、逐部分顶点网格表示和多阶段结构化接触推理，在无需训练的情况下超越了以往监督方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MLLMs在视觉语义理解上表现出色，但将其用于密集手部接触估计面临两个挑战：1) MLLMs难以编码显式3D手部几何；2) MLLMs倾向于关注高层语义而非细粒度几何推理，难以捕捉顶点级别的接触。

Method: 1) 引入详细的手部分割和逐部分顶点网格表示，将3D手部几何结构化为局部化几何信息；2) 设计多阶段结构化接触推理（带部分条件），逐步从全局语义过渡到细粒度几何推理。

Result: 无需任何训练，在密集手部接触估计任务上超越了以往在大规模数据集上训练的有监督方法。

Conclusion: ContactPrompt有效利用MLLMs的推理能力，实现了精确的密集手部接触估计，证明了零样本方法在几何推理任务上的潜力。

Abstract: Dense hand contact estimation requires both high-level semantic understanding and fine-grained geometric reasoning of human interaction to accurately localize contact regions. Recently, multi-modal large language models (MLLMs) have demonstrated strong capabilities in understanding visual semantics, enabled by vision-language priors learned from large-scale data. However, leveraging MLLMs for dense hand contact estimation remains underexplored. There are two major challenges in applying MLLMs to dense hand contact estimation. First, encoding explicit 3D hand geometry is difficult, as MLLMs primarily operate on vision and language modalities. Second, capturing fine-grained vertex-level contact remains challenging, as MLLMs tend to focus on high-level semantics rather than detailed geometric reasoning. To address these challenges, we propose ContactPrompt, a training-free and zero-shot approach for dense hand contact estimation using MLLMs. To effectively encode 3D hand geometry, we introduce a detailed hand-part segmentation and a part-wise vertex-grid representation that provides structured, localized geometric information. To enable accurate and efficient dense contact prediction, we develop a multi-stage structured contact reasoning with part conditioning, progressively bridging global semantics and fine-grained geometry. Therefore, our method effectively leverages the reasoning capabilities of MLLMs while enabling precise dense hand contact estimation. Surprisingly, the proposed approach outperforms previous supervised methods trained on large-scale dense contact datasets without requiring any training. The codes will be released.

</details>


### [50] [DBMSolver: A Training-free Diffusion Bridge Sampler for High-Quality Image-to-Image Translation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05889)
*Sankarshana Venugopal,Mohammad Mostafavi,Jonghyun Choi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DBMSolver是一种无需训练的扩散桥模型采样器，通过指数积分器利用半线性结构，将所需函数评估次数减少高达5倍，同时提升图像生成质量（如FID降低53%），在多种图像到图像翻译任务中实现了新的效率-质量最优权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的扩散桥模型（DBMs）在图像到图像翻译中虽然生成质量高，但采样速度慢，需要大量函数评估（NFEs），限制了实际应用。作者旨在通过无需训练的采样器加速DBMs的采样过程，同时保持甚至提升生成质量。

Method: 提出DBMSolver，一种无需训练的采样器，利用指数积分器（exponential integrators）挖掘DBM底层SDE和ODE的半线性结构，设计出一阶和二阶的高效求解方案，从而大幅减少所需的函数评估次数。

Result: NFEs减少高达5倍，同时质量提升：在DIODE数据集上20 NFEs下FID相比二阶基线下降53%。在图像修复、风格化、语义到图像生成等任务上（分辨率达256x256），DBMSolver实现了新的效率-质量最优权衡。

Conclusion: DBMSolver通过无需训练的采样器显著加速了扩散桥模型的采样过程，证明了其在多种图像到图像翻译任务中的实用性和优越性，代码已开源。

Abstract: Diffusion-based image-to-image (I2I) translation excels in high-fidelity generation but suffers from slow sampling in state-of-the-art Diffusion Bridge Models (DBMs), often requiring dozens of function evaluations (NFEs). We introduce DBMSolver, a training-free sampler that exploits the semi-linear structure of DBM's underlying SDE and ODE via exponential integrators, yielding highly-efficient 1st- and 2nd-order solutions. This reduces NFEs by up to 5x while boosting quality (e.g., FID drops 53% on DIODE at 20 NFEs vs. 2nd-order baseline). Experiments on inpainting, stylization, and semantics-to-image tasks across resolutions up to 256x256 show DBMSolver sets new SOTA efficiency-quality tradeoffs, enabling real-world applicability. Our code is publicly available at https://github.com/snumprlab/dbmsolver.

</details>


### [51] [MTL-MAD: Multi-Task Learners are Effective Medical Anomaly Detectors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05891)
*Bogdan Alexandru Bercean,Florinel Alin Croitoru,Vlad Hondru,Ciprian Mihai Ceausescu,Andreea Iuliana Ionescu,Radu Tudor Ionescu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出基于混合专家模型的多任务学习方法，通过联合多个自监督和伪标签任务进行医学图像异常检测，在BMAD基准上超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医学图像异常检测中，异常样本通常不可用于训练；现有方法多依赖单一预训练任务，本文探索如何通过多任务联合学习更好地建模正常解剖结构。

Method: 提出基于Mixture-of-Experts（MoE）的联合模型，从零开始学习多个自监督和伪标签任务，通过多任务学习器在推理时评估各任务解决程度来推导异常分数。

Result: 在涵盖多种医学图像模态的BMAD基准上，该方法优于所有最先进的竞争对手，并生成可解释的异常热图。

Conclusion: 多任务学习器是一种有效的异常检测方案，且具有可解释性，有助于辅助医生诊断。

Abstract: Anomaly detection in medical images is a challenging task, since anomalies are not typically available during training. Recent methods leverage a single pretext task coupled with a large-scale pre-trained model to reach state-of-the-art performance. Instead, we propose to learn multiple self-supervised and pseudo-labeling tasks from scratch, using a joint model based on Mixture-of-Experts (MoE). By carefully integrating multiple proxy tasks, the joint model effectively learns a robust representation of normal anatomical structures, so that anomaly scores can be derived based on how well the multi-task learner (MTL) solves each task during inference. We perform comprehensive experiments on BMAD, a recent benchmark that comprises a broad range of medical image modalities. The empirical results indicate that our multi-task learner is an effective anomaly detector, outperforming all state-of-the-art competitors on BMAD. Moreover, our model produces interpretable anomaly maps, potentially helping physicians in providing more accurate diagnoses.

</details>


### [52] [Detecting AI-Generated Videos with Spiking Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05895)
*Minsuk Jang,Yujin Yang,Heeseon Kim,Minseok Son,Younghun Kim,Changick Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出MAST检测器，利用脉冲神经网络(SNN)处理多通道时间残差，联合冻结语义编码器，在跨生成器AI视频检测任务上达到93.14%平均准确率，揭示了AI生成视频在像素级和语义级均存在时间平滑性差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI视频检测器在跨生成器场景下性能急剧下降，因为不同生成器的伪影类型和时间尺度各异。作者发现AI生成视频在像素级帧间残差更平滑、语义特征轨迹更紧凑，且SNN对边缘处的时间伪影有特殊响应模式，这些线索未被现有方法联合利用。

Method: 提出MAST（Multi-channel residual with Spike-driven Temporal branch），包含两个分支：(1) 脉冲驱动的时间分支，使用SNN处理多通道帧间残差，利用SNN的事件驱动、稀疏激活特性捕捉局部边缘处的时序伪影；(2) 冻结的语义编码器，提取全局语义特征。两者联合实现跨生成器泛化。

Result: 在GenVideo基准上，跨10个未见生成器的严格评估下，MAST达到93.14%平均准确率，匹配或超越最强的ANN检测器，证明了SNN在AI视频检测中的实际可行性。

Conclusion: AI生成视频在像素级和语义级均存在时间平滑性差距；SNN由于其事件驱动、稀疏激活的特性，天然适合捕捉集中在变化边缘处的时序伪影，为跨生成器泛化提供了新思路。

Abstract: Modern AI-generated videos are photorealistic at the single-frame level, leaving inter-frame dynamics as the main remaining axis for detection. Existing detectors typically handle this temporal evidence in three ways: feeding the full frame sequence to a generic temporal backbone, reducing one dominant temporal cue to fixed video-level descriptors, or comparing temporal features to real-video statistics through a detection metric. These strategies degrade sharply under cross-generator evaluation, where artifact type and timescale vary across generators. On caption-paired benchmark, GenVidBench, we identify two signatures that prior detectors do not jointly exploit: AI-generated videos exhibit smoother frame-to-frame temporal residuals at the pixel level, and more compact trajectories in the semantic feature space, indicating a temporal smoothness gap at both levels. We further observe that, when raw video is fed into a Spiking Neural Networks (SNNs), fake clips elicit firing predominantly at object and motion boundaries, unlike real clips, suggesting that the SNN responds to temporal artifacts localized at edges. These cues are sparse, asynchronous, and concentrated at moments of change, which makes SNNs a natural choice for this task: their event-driven, sparsely-activated dynamics align with the structure of the residual signal in a way that dense ANN backbones do not. Building on this observation, we propose MAST, a detector that processes multi-channel temporal residuals with a spike-driven temporal branch alongside a frozen semantic encoder for cross-generator generalization. On the GenVideo benchmark, MAST achieves 93.14\% mean accuracy across 10 unseen generators under strict cross-generator evaluation, matching or surpassing the strongest ANN-based detectors and demonstrating the practical applicability of SNNs to AI-generated video detection.

</details>


### [53] [Understanding Cross-Language Transfer Improvements in Low-Resource HTR: The Role of Sequence Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05900)
*Sana Al-azzawi,Chang Liu,Nudrat Habib,Elisa Barney,Marcus Liwicki*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文通过在阿拉伯语、乌尔都语和波斯语三种低资源场景下的对比实验，发现跨语言手写文本识别的性能提升主要来自序列级建模（CRNN），而非共享视觉表征（CNN alone）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究阿拉伯语手写文本识别中跨语言联合训练效果提升的来源：是共享视觉表征还是序列级依赖。

Method: 在KHATT（阿拉伯语）、NUST-UHWR（乌尔都语）和PHTD（波斯语）三个数据集上，控制变量对比CNN-only+CTC与CRNN模型在单脚本和多脚本训练下的性能差异，设置K={100,500,1000}三种低资源条件。

Result: CNN-only模型在多脚本训练下提升有限且不稳定；CRNN模型在多脚本训练下表现更优，尤其在数据最稀缺时效果显著。跨语言提升与序列级建模相关，而共享视觉表征本身不足以带来有效迁移。

Conclusion: 上下文建模（序列级依赖）在低资源场景下对跨语言迁移起关键作用，仅共享视觉特征是不够的；该发现可能推广到其他低资源语言场景。

Abstract: Handwritten Text Recognition (HTR) for Arabic-script languages benefits from cross-language joint training under low-resource conditions, particularly when using CRNN-based models that combine convolutional encoders with sequence modeling. However, it remains unclear whether these improvements are better explained by shared visual representations or sequence-level dependencies. In this work, we conduct a controlled architectural study of line-level Arabic-script HTR, comparing CNN-only models with CTC decoding and CRNN models under identical single-script and multi-script training regimes. Experiments are performed on Arabic (KHATT), Urdu (NUST-UHWR), and Persian (PHTD) datasets under low-resource settings (K in {100, 500, 1000}). Our results show a clear divergence in transfer behavior: while CNN-only models exhibit limited or unstable improvements, CRNN models achieve better performance under multi-script training, particularly in the most data-constrained regimes. Focusing on transfer improvements (delta CER) rather than absolute performance, we find that cross-language improvements are associated with sequence-level modeling, while sharing visual representations learned by the CNN encoder, corresponding to similarities in character shapes across scripts, alone appears to be insufficient. This finding suggests that contextual modeling plays an important role in enabling effective transfer in low-resource scenarios, and that similar behavior may extend to other low-resource language settings.

</details>


### [54] [Architecture-agnostic Lipschitz-constant Bayesian header and its application to resolve semantically proximal classification errors with vision transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05908)
*Frederik Schäfer,Luis Mandl,Lars Kälber,Tim Ricken*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种与架构无关的Lipschitz约束贝叶斯头部模块(LipB-ViT)，通过谱归一化变分权重的均值和log方差来增强模型在结构化标签噪声下的鲁棒性，结合自适应融合方案和新型评估指标，在语义错误标签检测中召回率超过0.93，比现有方法提升7%以上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标签噪声（特别是结构化语义近似的分类错误）严重制约了监督深度学习模型的泛化能力，而现有的鲁棒训练方法在处理这种噪声时往往失效。

Method: 提出了一种与架构无关的Lipschitz常数贝叶斯头部模块，对变分权重的均值和log方差施加谱归一化（bi-Lipschitz约束），并设计了自适应算术平均融合方案，结合特征空间邻近性与预测不确定性来检测被污染的标签。

Result: 在15%语义错误标签场景下，召回率超过0.93，比最先进的k近邻识别方法提升7%以上；在结构化/非结构化噪声推理场景下均展现出鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 该方法与预训练骨干网络即插即用、超参数跨领域一致，虽因蒙特卡洛采样增加计算成本，但在标注可靠性多变的high-stakes应用中具有显著实用价值。

Abstract: Label noise remains a critical bottleneck for the generalization of supervised deep learning models, particularly when errors are structured rather than random. Standard robust training methods often fail in the presence of such semantically proximal classification errors. This work presents an architecture-agnostic Lipschitz-constant Bayesian header that can be integrated into feature extractors such as vision transformers, yielding the bi-Lipschitz-constrained Bayesian Vision Transformer (LipB-ViT). In contrast to conventional Bayesian layers, our approach enforces spectral normalization on both the mean and log-variance of the variational weights, which promotes calibrated predictive uncertainty and mitigates noise amplification. We further propose a novel metric to jointly capture uncertainty and confidence across misclassification rates, as well as an adaptive arithmetic-mean fusion scheme that combines feature-space proximity with predictive uncertainty to detect corrupted labels outperforming the state of the art k-nearest neighbor based identification methods by more than 7% reaching a recall of more than 0.93 at 15% semantically misclassified labels. Although computational costs increase due to Monte Carlo sampling, the method offers plug-and-play compatibility with pre-trained backbones and consistent hyperparameters across domains, suggesting strong utility for high-stakes applications with variable annotation reliability. The stabilized confidence estimates serve as the foundation for an analysis pipeline that jointly assesses dataset quality and label noise, yielding a second novel metric for their combined quantification. Lastly, we systematically evaluate LipB-ViT under both structured (adversarial) and unstructured noise at inference time, demonstrating its robustness in realistic high-noise and attack scenarios. We compare its performance against baseline methods.

</details>


### [55] [Plug-and-play Class-aware Knowledge Injection for Prompt Learning with Visual-Language Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05910)
*Junhui Yin,Nan Pu,Xinyu Zhang,Lingfeng Yang,Lin Wu,Xiaojie Wang,Zhun Zhong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出CAKI框架，通过类感知知识注入（类特定提示生成+查询-键提示匹配）增强现有提示学习方法，在零样本分类中同时利用类共享、类特定和实例特定信息的优势，提升基类和新类性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有提示学习方法要么使用类共享提示（忽略类间细粒度区分），要么使用实例特定提示（忽略同类数据间的类级信息），导致分类次优。本文旨在通过注入类特定知识来弥补这一空白。

Method: 提出CAKI框架，包含两个核心组件：(1) 类特定提示生成——从少量同类样本中编码类特定知识，存入类级知识库；(2) 查询-键提示匹配——为每个测试实例从知识库中检索相关类级知识并注入，以精炼模型预测。该框架为即插即用型，可增强现有方法。

Result: 大量实验表明CAKI在基类和新类上均有效提升了现有方法的性能。

Conclusion: 类特定知识是现有提示学习方法中不可或缺的补充信号，通过即插即用的CAKI框架注入该类知识可显著提升VLM零样本分类性能。

Abstract: Prompt learning has become an effective and widely used technique in enhancing vision-language models (VLMs) such as CLIP for various downstream tasks, particularly in zero-shot classification within specific domains. Existing methods typically focus on either learning class-shared prompts for a given domain or generating instance-specific prompts through conditional prompt learning. While these methods have achieved promising performance, they often overlook class-specific knowledge in prompt design, leading to suboptimal outcomes. The underlying reasons are: 1) class-specific prompts offer more fine-grained supervision compared to coarse class-shared prompts, which helps prevent misclassification of data from different classes into a single class; 2) compared to class-specific prompts, instance-specific prompts neglect the richer class-level information across multiple instances, potentially causing data from the same class to be divided into multiple classes. To effectively supplement the class-specific knowledge into existing methods, we propose a plug-and-play Class-Aware Knowledge Injection (CAKI) framework. CAKI comprises two key components, i.e., class-specific prompt generation and query-key prompt matching. The former encodes class-specific knowledge into prompts from few-shot samples that belong to the same class and stores the learned prompts in a class-level knowledge bank. The latter provides a plug-and-play mechanism for each test instance to retrieve relevant class-level knowledge from the knowledge bank and inject such knowledge to refine model predictions. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our CAKI effectively improves the performance of existing methods on base and novel classes. Code is publicly available at \href{https://github.com/yjh576/CAKI}{this https URL}.

</details>


### [56] [Think, then Score: Decoupled Reasoning and Scoring for Video Reward Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05922)
*Yuan Wang,Ouxiang Li,Yulong Xu,Borui Liao,Jiajun Liang,Jinghan Li,Meng Wang,Xintao Wang,Pengfei Wang,Kuien Liu,Xiang Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出DeScore，一种解耦的"先思考后评分"视频奖励模型，通过两阶段训练框架结合CoT推理与判别式评分，在保持泛化能力的同时解决生成式奖励模型的训练不稳定问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频奖励模型面临两难：判别式RM缺乏显式推理易发生捷径学习，严重依赖数据规模；生成式RM虽具有更好的可解释性和泛化潜力，但推理与评分耦合在单一自回归链中导致优化瓶颈。

Method: 提出DeScore，采用解耦的"think-then-score"范式：MLLM先生成显式CoT，再由可学习查询标记和回归头组成的判别式评分模块预测最终奖励。两阶段优化框架：(1) 判别式冷启动（含随机掩码机制）确保稳健评分能力；(2) 双目标强化学习阶段，独立优化CoT推理质量和校准最终奖励。

Result: DeScore在保持CoT推理泛化优势的同时，缓解了推理与评分耦合带来的训练不稳定性，实现训练高效且泛化能力强的视频奖励模型。

Conclusion: 解耦的think-then-score范式能够有效结合生成式RM的泛化能力与判别式RM的训练稳定性，高质量推理可直接转化为更优的模型性能。

Abstract: Recent advances in generative video models are increasingly driven by post-training and test-time scaling, both of which critically depend on the quality of video reward models (RMs). An ideal reward model should predict accurate rewards that align with human preferences across diverse scenarios. However, existing paradigms face a fundamental dilemma: \textit{Discriminative RMs} regress rewards directly on features extracted by multimodal large language models (MLLMs) without explicit reasoning, making them prone to shortcut learning and heavily reliant on massive data scaling for generalization. In contrast, \textit{Generative RMs} with Chain-of-Thought (CoT) reasoning exhibit superior interpretability and generalization potential, as they leverage fine-grained semantic supervision to internalize the rationales behind human preferences. However, they suffer from inherent optimization bottlenecks due to the coupling of reasoning and scoring within a single autoregressive inference chain. To harness the generalization benefits of CoT reasoning while mitigating the training instability of coupled reasoning and scoring, we introduce DeScore, a training-efficient and generalizable video reward model. DeScore employs a decoupled ``think-then-score'' paradigm: an MLLM first generates an explicit CoT, followed by a dedicated discriminative scoring module consisting of a learnable query token and a regression head that predicts the final reward. DeScore is optimized via a two-stage framework: (1) a discriminative cold start incorporating a random mask mechanism to ensure robust scoring capabilities, and (2) a dual-objective reinforcement learning stage that independently refines CoT reasoning quality and calibrates the final reward, ensuring that higher-quality reasoning directly translates to superior model performance.

</details>


### [57] [Backdoor Mitigation in Object Detection via Adversarial Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05928)
*Kealan Dunnett,Reza Arablouei,Dimity Miller,Volkan Dedeoglu,Raja Jurdak*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种检测感知的对抗微调框架，用于在仅访问受损检测器和少量干净数据的情况下缓解目标检测后门攻击，通过软分支最小化和双重目标微调损失，有效降低攻击成功率并保持干净检测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有后门防御主要针对图像分类任务，而目标检测的后门防御研究不足。直接将分类领域的对抗微调方法迁移到检测任务面临挑战：分类导向的对抗生成不匹配检测攻击空间（攻击可能导致物体误分类或消失），且标准检测损失会稀释修复信号。

Method: 1) 提出软分支最小化（soft-branch minimisation），通过软门控结合误分类和消失攻击目标，并引入检测感知的分类损失最大化进行对抗生成，无需知晓攻击目标。2) 提出双重目标微调损失，应用于目标匹配的预测框，集中防御更新于与后门行为最相关的预测上。

Result: 在CNN和Transformer检测器上的实验表明，与分类导向基线相比，该方法更有效地降低了攻击成功率，同时保持了干净的检测性能。

Conclusion: 本文提出的检测感知对抗微调框架有效填补了目标检测后门防御的空白，通过软分支最小化和双重目标微调损失，在有限数据和未知攻击目标条件下实现了有效的后门缓解。

Abstract: Backdoor attacks can implant malicious behaviours into deep models while preserving performance on clean data, posing a serious threat to safety-critical vision systems. Although backdoor mitigation has been studied extensively for image classification, defenses for object detection remain comparatively underdeveloped. Adversarial fine-tuning is a common backdoor mitigation approach in classification, but adapting it to detection is nontrivial as classification-oriented adversarial generation does not match the detection attack space, where attacks may cause object misclassification or disappearance, and standard detection losses can dilute the repair signal across many predictions. We address these challenges through a detection-aware adversarial fine-tuning framework for mitigating object-detection backdoors when the defender has access only to a compromised detector and a small clean dataset, without knowing the attack objective. For adversarial generation that does not require knowledge of the attack objective, we introduce soft-branch minimisation, which uses a soft gate to combine objectives aligned with misclassification and disappearance attacks, together with a detection-aware classification-loss maximisation. For targeted repair, we introduce a dual-objective fine-tuning loss applied to target-matched predictions, concentrating the defensive update on predictions most relevant to the backdoor behaviour. Experiments across CNN- and Transformer-based detectors show that our approach more effectively reduces attack success while preserving true detections, compared with classification-oriented baselines, and maintains competitive clean detection performance.

</details>


### [58] [Whole-body CT attenuation and volume charts from routine clinical scans via evidence-grounded LLM report filtering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05933)
*Christian Wachinger,Bernhard Renger,Christopher Späth,Jan Kirschke,Marcus Makowski*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文开发了一种基于证据的大语言模型集成方法，从放射报告中过滤病理发现，构建了超过35万例CT检查的病理减少队列，并建立了涵盖106个解剖结构的全身参考图表，支持标准化的定量表型分析和机会性筛查研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解释定量CT生物标志物需要大规模健康参考分布，但临床数据集通常富含病理信息，难以构建此类分布。

Method: 采用五种大语言模型集成，首先基于报告证据标记结构级异常候选，然后通过交叉验证解决分歧；使用分布感知的GAMLSS模型建立全身参考图表，涵盖年龄、性别、造影增强和采集参数等因素。

Result: 建立了106个解剖结构（体积和衰减）的全身参考图表；纵向分析揭示了与横断面趋势不同的结构和造影依赖性变化。

Conclusion: 这些资源有助于协变量调整的百分位数评分，支持标准化定量表型分析、多站点影像研究和可扩展的机会性筛查研究。

Abstract: Interpreting quantitative CT biomarkers, such as organ volume and tissue attenuation, requires large-scale healthy reference distributions. However, creating these is challenging because clinical datasets are often heavily enriched with pathology. Here, we develop an evidence-grounded, cross-verified large language model (LLM) ensemble to filter pathological findings from radiology reports, enabling the construction of pathology-reduced cohorts from over 350,000 CT examinations. Five LLMs, first, flag structure-level abnormality candidates grounded in verbatim report evidence and, second, resolve disagreements via cross-verification. Using distribution-aware generalized additive models for location, scale, and shape, we establish comprehensive whole-body reference charts for 106 anatomical structures (volumes and attenuation) across adulthood, accounting for age, sex, contrast enhancement, and acquisition parameters. Longitudinal analyses reveal structure- and contrast-dependent changes distinct from cross-sectional trends. These resources facilitate covariate-adjusted centile scoring from routine CT, supporting standardized quantitative phenotyping, multi-site imaging studies, and scalable opportunistic screening research.

</details>


### [59] [RAWild: Sensor-Agnostic RAW Object Detection via Physics-Guided Curve and Grid Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05941)
*Shuhong Liu,Gengjia Chang,Jun Liu,Xuangeng Chu,Yinqiang Zheng,Tatsuya Harada,Ziteng Cui*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出RAWild，一种物理引导的全局-局部色调映射框架，用于传感器无关的RAW图像目标检测。通过全局色调校正和空间自适应局部颜色调整，结合物理仿真管线，实现了跨异构传感器的联合训练，在多个RAW基准上达到SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RAW数据相比sRGB具有更深位深、保留物理信息、无ISP畸变等优势，但不同传感器间的曝光条件、光谱灵敏度和位深差异导致较大的域间隙，使得传感器无关的泛化成为基本挑战。

Method: 提出RAWild框架，包含：(1) 物理引导的全局色调校正，将传感器引起的差异分解为全局色调校正和空间自适应局部颜色调整；(2) 两者均由RAW分布先验驱动；(3) 构建基于物理的RAW仿真管线，合成覆盖多样光谱灵敏度、光源和传感器非理想性的真实传感器输出。

Result: 在覆盖10到24位深度的多个RAW基准上，在单数据集、混合数据集和鲁棒性设置下均达到SOTA性能。

Conclusion: RAWild通过物理引导的色调映射框架有效解决了跨传感器RAW目标检测的域泛化问题，验证了RAW数据在目标检测中的潜力。

Abstract: Camera sensor RAW data offers intrinsic advantages for object detection, including deeper bit depth, preserved physical information, and freedom from image signal processor (ISP) distortions. However, varying exposure conditions, spectral sensitivities, and bit depths across devices introduce substantially larger domain gaps than sRGB, making sensor-agnostic generalization a fundamental challenge. In this study, we present \textbf{RAWild}, a physics-guided global-local tone mapping framework for sensor-agnostic RAW object detection. By factoring sensor-induced variations into a global tonal correction and a spatially adaptive local color adjustment, both driven by RAW distribution priors, our framework enables a single network to train jointly across heterogeneous sensors. To further support cross-sensor generalization, we construct a physics-based RAW simulation pipeline that synthesizes realistic sensor outputs spanning diverse spectral sensitivities, illuminants, and sensor non-idealities. Extensive experiments across multiple RAW benchmarks covering bit depths from 10 to 24 demonstrate state-of-the-art (SOTA) performance under single-dataset, mixed-dataset, and challenging robustness settings.

</details>


### [60] [MobileEgo Anywhere: Open Infrastructure for long horizon egocentric data on commodity hardware](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05945)
*Senthil Palanisamy,Abhishek Anand,Satpal Singh Rathor,Pratyush Patnaik,Shubhanshu Khatana*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出MobileEgo Anywhere框架，利用智能手机传感器收集超长时程（小时级）的第一人称机器人操作数据，发布200小时数据集、开源手机应用和标准化处理流程，以降低VLA模型训练数据的采集门槛。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型依赖的第一人称数据集时长太短（仅几分钟），无法捕捉复杂机器人任务所需的长程时间依赖关系，且传统数据采集硬件门槛高。

Method: 利用现代智能手机的通用传感器套件进行高保真、长时程相机位姿追踪；开发手机APP供用户录制第一人称数据；设计标准化处理流程将原始数据转为VLA模型可训练格式。

Result: 发布了200小时多样化长时程第一人称数据集，并提供开源手机应用和完整数据处理管线。

Conclusion: 该方法降低了数据采集的硬件门槛，使大规模长时程数据的获取变得民主化，有助于推动泛化机器人策略的研发。

Abstract: The recent advancement of Vision Language Action (VLA) models has driven a critical demand for large scale egocentric datasets. However, existing datasets are often limited by short episode durations, typically spanning only a few minutes, which fails to capture the long horizon temporal dependencies necessary for complex robotic task execution. To bridge this gap, we present MobileEgo Anywhere, a framework designed to facilitate the collection of robust, hour plus egocentric trajectories using commodity mobile hardware. We leverage the ubiquitous sensor suites of modern smartphones to provide high fidelity, long term camera pose tracking, effectively removing the high hardware barriers associated with traditional robotics data collection. Our contributions are three fold: (1) we release a novel dataset comprising 200 hours of diverse, long form egocentric data with persistent state tracking; (2) we open source a mobile application that enables any user to record egocentric data, and (3) we provide a comprehensive processing pipeline to convert raw mobile captures into standardized, training ready formats for Vision Language Action model and foundation model research. By democratizing the data collection process, this work enables the massive scale acquisition of long horizon data across varied global environments, accelerating the development of generalizable robotic policies.

</details>


### [61] [Prompt-Free and Efficient SAM2 Adaptation for Biomedical Semantic Segmentation via Dual Adapters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05979)
*Hinako Mitsuoka,Kazuhiro Hotta*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 针对SAM2在生物医学分割中领域偏移和提示依赖的问题，提出了一种无提示、参数高效的微调框架，采用卷积位置编码生成器和双适配器策略，在多个医学数据集上显著提升分割精度并降低计算成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SAM2在自然图像上表现优异，但在生物医学分割中面临严重的领域偏移和提示依赖问题，需要一种无需提示且参数高效的适配方法。

Method: 提出无提示、参数高效的微调框架，包含：(1) 卷积位置编码生成器适应任意长宽比输入；(2) 双适配器策略——高性能适配器使用可变形卷积精确建模边界，轻量适配器采用结构重参数化降低推理延迟。

Result: 在ISBI 2012、Kvasir-SEG、Synapse、ACDC数据集上显著优于强基线，相比原始SAM2分割精度最高提升19.66%，计算成本相比重型医学SAM适配降低约87%。

Conclusion: 该方法在精度与效率之间建立了优越的平衡，有效解决了SAM2在医学分割中的领域偏移和提示依赖问题。

Abstract: Segment Anything Model 2 (SAM2) demonstrated impressive zero-shot capabilities on natural images but faces challenges in biomedical segmentation due to significant domain shifts and prompt dependency. To address these limitations, we propose a prompt-free, parameter-efficient fine-tuning framework designed for multi-class segmentation on variable-sized inputs. We introduce a convolutional Positional Encoding Generator to adapt effectively to arbitrary aspect ratios and present a dual-adapter strategy: High-Performance Adapter utilizing deformable convolutions for precise boundary modeling and Lightweight Adapter employing structural re-parameterization to minimize inference latency. Experiments on ISBI 2012, Kvasir-SEG, Synapse, and ACDC datasets demonstrate that our approach significantly outperforms strong adaptation baselines. Specifically, our method improved segmentation accuracy by up to 19.66\% over the vanilla SAM2, while reducing computational costs by approximately 87\% compared to heavyweight medical SAM adaptations, establishing a superior trade-off between accuracy and efficiency.

</details>


### [62] [iPhoneBlur: A Difficulty-Stratified Benchmark for Consumer Device Motion Deblurring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05990)
*Abdullah Al Shafi,Kazi Saeed Alam*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: iPhoneBlur是一个基于iPhone 17 Pro高帧率视频合成的难度分层运动模糊恢复基准（7,400对图像），按难易程度分为Easy/Medium/Hard三档，揭示了传统聚合指标掩盖的7-9 dB性能差距，并暴露了专业相机与消费级相机之间的领域差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有运动模糊恢复评估使用聚合指标，掩盖了不同难度下的性能差异，无法反映模型在真实部署场景中的行为。

Method: 从iPhone 17 Pro高帧率视频合成7,400对模糊/清晰图像，通过PSNR引导的自适应时间窗口将样本分为Easy/Medium/Hard三档，并提供ISP感知等元数据。

Result: 六种架构在Easy到Hard子集上表现出一致的7-9 dB性能下降，这一差距被聚合指标完全掩盖；同时揭示了专业相机与消费级相机之间的领域差距。

Conclusion: iPhoneBlur通过难度分层和元数据耦合，实现了对资源受限边缘系统中模型可靠性和故障模式的系统性评估。

Abstract: Motion blur restoration on consumer mobile devices is typically evaluated using aggregate metrics that obscure performance variation across blur difficulty, masking model behavior under real deployment conditions. This work introduces iPhoneBlur, a difficulty-stratified benchmark of 7,400 image pairs synthesized from high-framerate iPhone 17 Pro videos captured in diverse real-world scenarios. Samples are partitioned into Easy, Medium, and Hard categories through PSNR-guided adaptive temporal windowing, with stratification validated by monotonic 2.2x increase in optical flow magnitude across tiers. Each sample includes comprehensive metadata enabling investigation of ISP-aware and difficulty-adaptive restoration strategies. Spectral analysis confirms synthesized blur exhibits high-frequency suppression patterns consistent with authentic motion degradation. Evaluation of six architectures reveals consistent 7-9 dB performance degradation from Easy to Hard subsets, a substantial gap entirely hidden by aggregate reporting. The benchmark further exposes a domain gap between professional and consumer cameras which targeted fine-tuning substantially recovers. By coupling difficulty stratification with deployment-critical metadata, iPhoneBlur enables systematic assessment of model reliability and failure modes for resource-constrained edge systems.

</details>


### [63] [4DThinker: Thinking with 4D Imagery for Dynamic Spatial Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05997)
*Zhangquan Chen,Manyuan Zhang,Xinlei Yu,Xiang An,Bo Li,Xin Xie,ZiDong Wang,Mingze Sun,Shuang Chen,Hongyu Li,Xiaobin Hu,Ruqi Huang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出4DThinker，首个让视觉语言模型通过动态潜在心理意象进行4D时空推理的框架，包含无标注数据生成、动态意象微调(DIFT)和4D强化学习(4DRL)，在多项基准测试中超越强基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLM处理动态空间推理时，要么将时空推理完全文本化（冗长且不精确），要么依赖外部几何模块（增加复杂度且无益于模型内在能力提升），亟需让模型自身具备动态4D推理能力。

Method: 1) 可扩展的无标注数据生成管线，从原始视频合成4D推理数据；2) 动态意象微调(DIFT)，联合监督文本token和4D潜在表示；3) 4D强化学习(4DRL)，基于结果奖励优化复杂推理任务，仅对文本token施加策略梯度以保证稳定优化。

Result: 在多个动态空间推理基准上，4DThinker consistently outperforms strong baselines。

Conclusion: 4DThinker为VLM实现内在4D推理提供了新范式，证明了让模型在连续隐空间中进行动态心理模拟的可行性。

Abstract: Dynamic spatial reasoning from monocular video is essential for bridging visual intelligence and the physical world, yet remains challenging for vision-language models (VLMs). Prior approaches either verbalize spatial-temporal reasoning entirely as text, which is inherently verbose and imprecise for complex dynamics, or rely on external geometric modules that increase inference complexity without fostering intrinsic model capability. In this paper, we present 4DThinker, the first framework that enables VLMs to "think with 4D" through dynamic latent mental imagery, i.e., internally simulating how scenes evolve within the continuous hidden space. Specifically, we first introduce a scalable, annotation-free data generation pipeline that synthesizes 4D reasoning data from raw videos. We then propose Dynamic-Imagery Fine-Tuning (DIFT), which jointly supervises textual tokens and 4D latents to ground the model in dynamic visual semantics. Building on this, 4D Reinforcement Learning (4DRL) further tackles complex reasoning tasks via outcome-based rewards, restricting policy gradients to text tokens to ensure stable optimization. Extensive experiments across multiple dynamic spatial reasoning benchmarks demonstrate that 4DThinker consistently outperforms strong baselines and offers a new perspective toward 4D reasoning in VLMs. Our code is available at https://github.com/zhangquanchen/4DThinker.

</details>


### [64] [Neuromorphic visual attention for Sign-language recognition on SpiNNaker](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06005)
*Sarka Liskova,Olha Vedmedenko,Mazdak Fatahi,Matej Hoffmann,P. Michael Furlong,Giulia D Angelo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于神经形态感知和处理的端到端架构，用于美国手语指拼识别。该架构整合了脉冲视觉注意力机制实现在线区域提取，并结合紧凑型脉冲神经网络部署在SpiNNaker平台上。在合成和原生数据集上取得了竞争性性能，并在能耗（0.565 mW）和延迟（3 ms）方面实现了最优效果，验证了该架构在边缘部署的适用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有手语识别方法虽在分类准确率上取得进展，但延迟和功耗问题限制了其在实时场景中的部署。作者旨在利用神经形态感知与处理（基于稀疏事件驱动计算）的低延迟、低能耗优势，解决这一瓶颈。

Method: 提出端到端神经形态架构，包括：(1) 脉冲视觉注意力机制，用于在线感兴趣区域提取；(2) 紧凑型脉冲神经网络；(3) 部署在SpiNNaker神经形态硬件平台上。在合成事件版Sign Language MNIST和原生ASL-DVS数据集上进行评估。

Result: 仿真准确率92.27%，硬件部署准确率83.1%；功耗仅0.565 mW，延迟低至3 ms，在所有对比方法中能耗和延迟最优。

Conclusion: 尽管架构紧凑，该系统证明了任务依赖的视觉注意力应用在边缘部署上的可行性和有效性，为低功耗实时手语识别提供了新方向。

Abstract: Sign-language recognition has achieved substantial gains in classification accuracy in recent years; however, the latency and power requirements of most existing methods limit their suitability for real-time deployment. Neuromorphic sensing and processing offer an alternative paradigm based on sparse, event-driven computation that supports low-latency and energy-efficient perception. In this work, we introduce an end-to-end neuromorphic architecture for American Sign Language (ASL) fingerspelling recognition that integrates a spiking visual attention mechanism for online region-of-interest extraction with a compact spiking neural network deployed on the SpiNNaker neuromorphic platform. We benchmark the proposed system against two datasets: a synthetically generated event-based version of the Sign Language MNIST dataset and a natively recorded ASL-DVS dataset, whilst providing a comprehensive overview of Sign-language recognition and related work. This work yields competitive performance in simulation (92.27%) and comparable performance on neuromorphic hardware deployment (83.1%), while achieving the most energy-efficient architecture (0.565 mW) and low latency (3 ms) across all benchmarked approaches. Despite its compact design, the system demonstrates the suitability of task-dependent visual attention applications for edge deployment.

</details>


### [65] [Adding Thermal Awareness to Visual Systems in Real-Time via Distilled Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06010)
*Yuchen Guo,Junli Gong,Wenjun Dong,Yiuming Cheung,Weifeng Su*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 FusionProxy，一个即插即用的实时图像融合模块，通过利用教师样本集成的两种互补统计量（原始图像空间的逐像素方差和冻结基础骨干网络中的逐像素方差），实现扩散级别质量的 RGB-红外图像融合，在多种平台上达到实时推理速度，显著提升全天候感知系统的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 纯 RGB 视觉模型在夜间、雾天等挑战性场景下性能退化且存在安全隐患，而现有高质量红外融合方法延迟过高，无法在实时边缘设备上部署。

Method: FusionProxy 利用教师样本集成的两种统计量：(1) 原始图像空间的逐像素方差，用于加权像素级监督；(2) 冻结基础骨干网络中的逐像素方差，用于空间引导特征级对齐。该模块独立训练后可即插即用，无需联合优化。

Result: 在静态识别任务上达到最优性能，在动态任务（包括闭环自动驾驶）中显著增强鲁棒性，并在从高端 GPU 到低端硬件的多种平台上均实现实时推理速度。

Conclusion: FusionProxy 提供了一种灵活、可泛化的全天候感知解决方案，在保持扩散级别融合质量的同时实现实时部署，适用于各类视觉感知系统。

Abstract: Purely RGB-based vision models often fail to provide reliable cues in challenging scenarios such as nighttime and fog, leading to degraded performance and safety risks. Infrared imaging captures heat-emitting sources and provides critical complementary information, but existing high-fidelity fusion methods suffer from prohibitive latency, rendering them impractical for real-time edge deployment. To address this, we propose FusionProxy, a real-time image fusion module designed as a fully independent, plug-and-play component with diffusion level quality. FusionProxy exploits two complementary statistics of a teacher sample ensemble: per-pixel variance in raw image space, used to weight pixel-level supervision, and per-pixel variance inside frozen foundation backbones, used to route feature-level alignment spatially. Once trained, FusionProxy can be directly integrated into any visual perception system without joint optimization. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our method achieves superior performance on static recognition tasks and significantly enhances robustness in dynamic tasks, including closed-loop autonomous driving. Crucially, FusionProxy achieves real-time inference speeds on diverse platforms, from high-end GPUs to commodity hardware, providing a flexible and generalizable solution for all-day perception.

</details>


### [66] [T2I-VeRW: Part-level Fine-grained Perception for Text-to-Image Vehicle Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06012)
*Xiao Wang,Ziwen Wang,Weizhe Kong,Wentao Wu,Yuehang Li,Aihua Zheng,Chenglong Li,Jin Tang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出PFCVR模型，通过部件级细粒度跨模态对齐和双向掩码恢复机制，实现文本到图像的车俩重识别，并构建了包含14,668张图像的新数据集T2I-VeRW，在两个基准上达到SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将车辆重识别从纯图像查询扩展到基于文本描述的真实场景检索，弥补现有方法缺乏部件级细粒度跨模态对齐的问题。

Method: 1) 构建部件级局部图文配对，引入可学习部件查询令牌，聚合部件特性和全文语境后与视觉部件特征对齐；2) 提出双向掩码恢复模块，在模态间引导下重建被掩码内容，将局部对应隐式桥接到全局特征对齐；3) 构建新数据集T2I-VeRW（14,668张图像，1,796个身份，含部件级标注）。

Result: T2I-VeRI数据集上Rank-1达29.2%（+3.7%），T2I-VeRW数据集上Rank-1达55.2%，均优于现有SOTA方法。

Conclusion: 通过部件级显式对齐和双向掩码恢复的隐式全局对齐，有效提升了文本到车辆重识别的性能，新数据集为后续研究提供基准。

Abstract: Vehicle Re-identification (Re-ID) aims to retrieve the most similar image to a given query from images captured by non-overlapping cameras. Extending vehicle Re-ID from image-only queries to text-based queries enables retrieval in real-world scenarios where only a witness description of the target vehicle is available. In this paper, we propose PFCVR, a Part-level Fine-grained Cross-modal Vehicle Retrieval model for text-to-image vehicle re-identification. PFCVR constructs locally paired images and texts at the part level and introduces learnable part-query tokens that aggregate both part-specific and full-sentence context before aligning with visual part features. On top of this explicit local alignment, a bi-directional mask recovery module lets each modality reconstruct its masked content under the guidance of the other, implicitly bridging local correspondences into global feature alignment. Furthermore, we construct a new large-scale dataset called T2I-VeRW, which contains 14,668 images covering 1,796 vehicle identities with fine-grained part-level annotations. Experimental results on the T2I-VeRI dataset show that PFCVR achieves 29.2\% Rank-1 accuracy, improving over the best competing method by +3.7\% percentage points. On the newly proposed T2I-VeRW benchmark, PFCVR achieves 55.2\% Rank-1 accuracy, outperforming a comprehensive set of recent state-of-the-art methods. Source code will be released on https://github.com/Event-AHU/Neuromorphic_ReID

</details>


### [67] [PlotPick: AI-powered batch extraction of numerical data from scientific figures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06021)
*Tommy Carstensen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PlotPick是一个利用视觉语言模型(VLMs)从科学图表中批量提取结构化表格数据的开源工具。在ChartX和PlotQA基准测试中，六种VLM均优于专用图表转表格模型DePlot，尤其是在DePlot训练数据中未出现的图表类型（如箱线图）上优势显著。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 系统综述和元分析经常需要数值数据，但作者通常只在图表中报告这些数据。手动数字化速度慢且难以规模化，因此需要自动化工具来高效提取图表中的表格数据。

Method: PlotPick利用视觉语言模型(VLMs)从科学图表中批量提取结构化表格数据。研究评估了来自三家提供商的六种VLM，在ChartX和PlotQA两个基准测试上进行比较，并与专用图表转表格模型DePlot进行对比。

Result: 所有六种VLM在ChartX和PlotQA基准测试上均优于DePlot。在ChartX上(限于柱状图、折线图、箱线图和直方图，n=300)，VLM达到88-96%的召回率，而DePlot为71%。在PlotQA上(n=529)，VLM达到86-99%的RMSF1，而DePlot为94%。在箱线图上差距最大：DePlot为24% RMSF1，而VLM达到83-97%。

Conclusion: 视觉语言模型在从科学图表中提取数据方面优于专用图表转表格模型DePlot，尤其是在DePlot训练数据中未出现的图表类型上表现突出。PlotPick作为开源工具可有效解决系统综述中的数据提取需求。

Abstract: Systematic reviews and meta-analyses frequently require numerical data that authors report only as figures, yet manual digitisation is slow and does not scale. We present PlotPick, an open-source tool that uses vision-language models (VLMs) to batch-extract structured tabular data from scientific figures. We evaluate six VLMs from three providers on two established chart-to-table benchmarks (ChartX and PlotQA) and compare against the dedicated chart-to-table model DePlot. All six VLMs outperform DePlot on both benchmarks. On ChartX (restricted to bar charts, line charts, box plots, and histograms; n=300), VLMs achieve 88-96% recall versus 71% for DePlot. On PlotQA (n=529), VLMs achieve 86-99% RMSF1 versus 94% for DePlot. The gap is largest on chart types absent from the dedicated models' training data: on box plots, DePlot achieves 24% RMSF1 while VLMs achieve 83-97%. PlotPick is available at https://plotpick.streamlit.app.

</details>


### [68] [Domain Generalization through Spatial Relation Induction over Visual Primitives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06043)
*Dat Nguyen,Duc-Duy Nguyen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PARSE通过将视觉识别分解为视觉基元及其关系组合，利用可微的空间对齐谓词实现端到端学习，在领域泛化任务上取得显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有领域泛化方法通过改进训练过程（如模型选择、数据增强、特征对齐）来寻求稳定表征，但这些方法隐式处理结构组合的表示学习，限制了在组合性领域泛化基准上的性能。

Method: 提出PARSE框架，包含三个组件：(1) CNN骨干网络提取通用视觉特征；(2) 概念瓶颈层将特征映射为基元热图及可微空间坐标；(3) 结构评分层评估检测到的基元间的候选空间关系。使用软二元、三元、四元谓词对基元位置关系进行组合表示，实现端到端学习。

Result: 在CUB-DG上准确率提升超过4.5个百分点，在DomainBed基准套件上与现有DG方法保持竞争力。

Conclusion: 显式建模视觉基元及其关系组合可以更有效地捕获跨域稳定的结构表征，提升组合性领域泛化性能。

Abstract: Domain generalization requires identifying stable representations that support reliable classification across domains. Most existing methods seek such stability through improving the training process, for example, through model selection strategies, data augmentation, or feature-alignment objectives. Although these strategies can be effective, they leave the representation learning of structural composition implicit, which may limit performance on compositional domain generalization benchmarks.
  In this work, we propose Primitive-Aware Relational Structure for domain gEneralization (PARSE), an image classification framework that factors visual recognition into visual primitives and their relational composition. We represent these compositions using soft binary, ternary, and quaternary predicates over primitive locations, yielding differentiable measures of spatial alignment that can be learned end-to-end. To learn primitives and relational structures jointly, we design an end-to-end architecture with three components: (1) a convolutional neural network (CNN) backbone that extracts general visual features, (2) a concept bottleneck layer that maps these features to primitive heatmaps with differentiable spatial coordinates, and (3) a structural scoring layer that evaluates candidate spatial relations among the detected primitives. We then compute class probability from the joint evidence of its class-specific relational compositions.
  Across CUB-DG and the DomainBed benchmark suite,PARSE improves accuracy by over 4.5 percentage points on CUB-DG and remains competitive with existing DG methods on DomainBed.

</details>


### [69] [Fusion in Your Way: Aligning Image Fusion with Heterogeneous Demands via Direct Preference Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06049)
*Weijian Su,Songqian Zhang,Yuqi Han,Jian Zhuang,Yongdong Huang,Qiang Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出DPOFusion框架，通过直接偏好优化（DPO）和潜在扩散模型实现红外与可见光图像的自适应融合，支持人类视觉和机器视觉的多样化偏好需求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有红外与可见光图像融合方法难以灵活适应异构需求（人类视觉与机器视觉偏好不同），实现自适应融合仍是一个开放挑战。

Method: 提出DPOFusion框架，包含两个模块：(1) 属性对齐潜在扩散模型(PALDM)，利用潜在融合先验和联合条件损失生成具有不同属性的多样化候选融合结果；(2) 偏好可控潜在扩散模型(PCLDM)，通过实例直接偏好优化(IDPO)微调，实现异构偏好信号对融合结果的直接控制。

Result: DPOFusion不仅在人类、视觉语言模型和任务驱动网络之间实现了精确的偏好对齐，还在自适应融合质量和任务导向可迁移性上树立了新基准。

Conclusion: DPOFusion通过偏好优化的方式有效解决了IVIF中人类与机器视觉的异构需求适配问题，实现了高质量的自适应融合与跨任务迁移。

Abstract: As a key technique in multi-modal processing, infrared and visible image fusion (IVIF) plays a crucial role in integrating complementary spectral information for visual enhancement and downstream vision tasks. Despite remarkable progress, existing methods struggle to flexibly accommodate heterogeneous demands. Achieving adaptive fusion that aligns with various preferences from both human and machine vision remains an open and challenging problem. To address this challenge, we propose DPOFusion, a direct preference optimization (DPO) framework integrating the property-aligned latent diffusion model (PALDM) and the preference-controllable latent diffusion model (PCLDM), enabling task-guided, preference-adaptive IVIF for both human and machine vision. The PALDM leverages a latent fusion prior and a joint conditional loss to generate diverse candidate fusion results with various properties. PCLDM is subsequently fine-tuned via instance direct preference optimization (IDPO), enabling direct control of the final fusion results with heterogeneous preference signals. Experimental results demonstrate that our framework not only attains precise preference alignment among humans, vision-language models, and task-driven networks, but also sets a new benchmark for adaptive fusion quality and task-oriented transferability.

</details>


### [70] [RealCam: Real-Time Novel-View Video Generation with Interactive Camera Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06051)
*Youcan Xu,Jiaxin Shi,Zhen Wang,Wensong Song,Feifei Shao,Chen Liang,Jun Xiao,Long Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出RealCam，一种自回归框架，通过跨帧上下文学习、分布匹配蒸馏和闭环数据增强，实现实时、交互式的摄像机可控视频生成，显著优于现有隐式方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有隐式视频生成方法依赖非因果的全序列处理和前缀式时间拼接，导致双向注意力计算延迟高、复杂度二次增长，无法支持实时流式或变长输入。

Method: (1) 设计基于跨帧上下文学习的高保真教师模型，将源帧与目标帧交织为同步上下文对，实现长度无关的因果适配；(2) 通过自强制与分布匹配蒸馏将教师蒸馏为少步因果学生，实现流式生成；(3) 提出闭环数据增强(LoopAug)，从多视图数据中合成全局一致的循环序列以缓解闭环不一致性。

Result: RealCam在视觉保真度和时间一致性上达到SOTA，推理速度比现有范式快数个数量级，支持真正的交互式摄像机控制。

Conclusion: RealCam通过自回归范式打破了隐式方法的瓶颈，实现了高效、实时的摄像机可控视频生成，为交互式电影制作和直播提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Camera-controlled video-to-video (V2V) generation enables dynamic viewpoint synthesis from monocular footage, holding immense potential for interactive filmmaking and live broadcasting. However, existing implicit synthesis methods fundamentally rely on non-causal, full-sequence processing and rigid prefix-style temporal concatenation. This architectural paradigm mandates bidirectional attention, resulting in prohibitive computational latency, quadratic complexity scaling, and inherent incompatibility with real-time streaming or variable-length inputs. To overcome these limitations, we introduce \texttt{RealCam}, a novel autoregressive framework for interactive, real-time camera-controlled V2V generation. We first design a high-fidelity teacher model grounded in a \textbf{Cross-frame In-context Learning} paradigm. By interleaving source and target frames into synchronized contextual pairs, our design inherently enables length-agnostic generalization and naturally facilitates causal adaptation, breaking the rigid prefix bottleneck. We then distill this teacher into a few-step causal student via Self-Forcing with Distribution Matching Distillation, enabling efficient, on-the-fly streaming synthesis. Furthermore, to mitigate severe loop inconsistency in closed-loop trajectories, we propose \textbf{Loop-Closed Data Augmentation (LoopAug)}, a novel paradigm that synthesizes globally consistent loop sequences from existing multiview datasets. Extensive experiments demonstrate that \texttt{RealCam} achieves state-of-the-art visual fidelity and temporal consistency while enabling truly interactive camera control with orders-of-magnitude faster inference than existing paradigms. Our project page is at https://xyc-fly.github.io/RealCam/.

</details>


### [71] [PersonaGesture: Single-Reference Co-Speech Gesture Personalization for Unseen Speakers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06064)
*Xiangyue Zhang,Yiyi Cai,Kunhang Li,Kaixing Yang,You Zhou,Zhengqing Li,Xuangeng Chu,Jiaxu Zhang,Haiyang Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出PersonaGesture，一个基于扩散模型的单参考共语手势个性化方法，通过自适应风格注入（ASI）和隐式分布修正（IDR）实现无需优化的新说话人手势生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有共语手势生成模型难以对新说话人进行个性化，因为参考动作混合了稳定的说话人习惯与特定语句的运动轨迹，且现有方法需要每个说话人单独优化，效率低。

Method: 包含两个核心组件：1) ASI（自适应风格注入）：Style Perceiver将变长参考编码为紧凑的说话人记忆token，通过零初始化的残差交叉注意力注入去噪过程；2) IDR（隐式分布修正）：在潜在空间中对参考的通道级矩进行长度感知的对角仿射映射修正。

Result: 在BEAT2和ZeroEGGS数据集上评估了定量指标、参考身份控制、同音频诊断、定性比较和人类偏好。实验表明分离去噪时的说话人记忆与后生成矩修正优于风格码塌缩、全参考注意力和单片段微调。

Conclusion: PersonaGesture有效解决了单参考共语手势个性化问题，通过解耦风格注入与分布修正，在不牺牲预训练先验的前提下实现了新说话人的手势风格迁移。

Abstract: We propose PersonaGesture, a diffusion-based pipeline for single-reference co-speech gesture personalization of unseen speakers. Given target speech and one motion clip from a new speaker, the model must synthesize gestures that follow the new utterance while retaining speaker-specific pose choices, without per-speaker optimization. This setting is useful for avatars and virtual agents, but it is hard because the reference mixes stable speaker habits with utterance-specific trajectories. PersonaGesture consists of two key components, Adaptive Style Infusion (ASI) and Implicit Distribution Rectification (IDR), to separate temporal identity evidence from residual statistic correction. A Style Perceiver first encodes the variable-length reference into compact speaker-memory tokens. ASI injects these tokens into denoising through zero-initialized residual cross-attention, enabling style evidence to affect motion formation without replacing the pretrained speech-to-motion prior. Building on this, IDR applies a length-aware diagonal affine map in latent space to correct residual channel-wise moments estimated from the same reference. Across BEAT2 and ZeroEGGS, we evaluate quantitative metrics, reference-identity controls, same-audio diagnostics, qualitative comparisons, and human preference. Experiments show that separating denoising-time speaker memory from conservative post-generation moment correction improves unseen-speaker personalization over collapsed style codes, full-reference attention, and one-clip finetuning. Project: https://xiangyue-zhang.github.io/PersonaGesture.

</details>


### [72] [Arena as Offline Reward: Efficient Fine-Grained Preference Optimization for Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06070)
*Zhikai Li,Yue Zhao,Edward Zhongwei Zhang,Xuewen Liu,Jing Zhang,Qingyi Gu,Zhen Dong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出ArenaPO方法，利用竞技场评分作为离线奖励，在不使用奖励模型的情况下实现文本到图像扩散模型的细粒度偏好优化，性能优于现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有DPO方法依赖二元反馈，只能在"选择-拒绝"对上进行粗粒度建模，优化效果欠佳。而传统RLHF虽能提供丰富奖励，但计算效率低。作者希望结合两者优势，实现高效且细粒度的优化。

Method: 1) 构建模型竞技场，将每个模型能力表示为高斯分布，通过标注的成对偏好推断能力分布。2) 对于图像对，基于两个能力分布和观测到的成对偏好，利用截断正态分布的潜变量推理估计绝对质量差距，作为细粒度训练信号。整个过程无需奖励模型，可离线计算。

Result: 在Pick-a-Pic v2和HPD v3数据集上，ArenaPO一致优于现有基线方法。

Conclusion: ArenaPO成功结合了传统RLHF的丰富奖励和DPO的高效性，通过竞技场评分提供细粒度反馈，实现了更优的偏好对齐效果。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning from human feedback (RLHF) effectively promotes preference alignment of text-to-image (T2I) diffusion models.
