### [182] [Chain of Risk: Safety Failures in Large Reasoning Models and Mitigation via Adaptive Multi-Principle Steering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05678)
*Xiaomin Li,Jianheng Hou,Zheyuan Deng,Zhiwei Zhang,Taoran Li,Binghang Lu,Bing Hu,Yunhan Zhao,Yuexing Hao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 大型推理模型（LRM）的推理轨迹中存在安全盲区：即使最终答案安全，推理过程中也可能出现有害内容。本文通过统一安全准则评估，发现推理轨迹比最终答案暴露更多安全风险，并提出自适应多原则引导（adaptive multi-principle steering）作为测试时缓解方法，在保持高准确率的同时显著降低不安全内容。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LRM暴露类思维链推理过程以增强透明性和可验证性，但这带来了安全盲点——有害或违规内容可能出现在推理轨迹中，即使最终答案看起来安全。现有安全评估仅关注最终答案，可能遗漏推理过程中的风险。

Method: 1) 使用统一20原则安全评分体系，对推理轨迹和最终答案分别打分；2) 从7个公害提示源和4个OOD源收集提示，评估15个LRM；3) 提出自适应多原则引导（adaptive multi-principle steering），在测试时学习每个安全原则的不安全→安全激活方向，仅在当前隐状态更接近不安全质心时激活引导。

Result: 推理轨迹持续暴露超出最终答案的额外安全风险，特别是高风险阶段失败（泄露案例和逃脱案例）。风险集中在错误信息、法律合规、歧视、身体伤害和心理伤害等原则。自适应引导在DeepSeek-R1-Qwen-7B上实现40.8%的平均不安全计数减少，同时保持97.7%的宏平均准确率。

Conclusion: LRM的安全性应在完整的推理-答案轨迹上进行评估和缓解，而非仅在最终答案阶段。

Abstract: Large reasoning models (LRMs) increasingly expose chain-of-thought-like reasoning for transparency, verification, and deliberate problem solving. This creates a safety blind spot: harmful or policy-violating content may appear in reasoning traces even when final answers appear safe. We test whether final-answer safety is a sufficient proxy for the full reasoning-answer trajectory by scoring both stages under a unified twenty-principle safety rubric. Using prompts from seven public harmfulness and jailbreak sources, plus four out-of-distribution (OOD) sources, we evaluate 15 open-weight and API-based LRMs across 41K prompts per model. Reasoning traces consistently reveal additional safety risks beyond final answers, especially in high-severity stage-wise failures: leak cases, where unsafe reasoning precedes a safe-looking answer, and escape cases, where benign-looking reasoning precedes an unsafe final response. Principle-level analysis shows that risk concentrates in misinformation, legal compliance, discrimination, physical harm, and psychological harm. We further propose adaptive multi-principle steering, a white-box test-time mitigation that learns one unsafe-to-safe activation direction per safety principle and activates only directions whose current hidden state is closer to the unsafe than safe centroid. On three steerable open reasoning models, adaptive steering reduces unsafe counts in both reasoning traces and final answers on held-out and OOD benchmarks. DeepSeek-R1-Qwen-7B achieves a 40.8% average unsafe-count reduction while retaining 97.7% macro-averaged accuracy on BBH, GSM8K, and MMLU. These results suggest that LRM safety should be evaluated and mitigated over the full exposed reasoning-answer trajectory, not only at the final-answer stage.

</details>


### [183] [Attractor Geometry of Transformer Memory: From Conflict Arbitration to Confident Hallucination](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05686)
*Qiyao Liang,Risto Miikkulainen,Ila Fiete*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文揭示了语言模型在参数记忆（PM）与工作记忆（WM）冲突及幻觉（未学到的事实）这两种失败模式下的统一几何机制：已学事实在隐空间形成吸引子盆地，冲突是盆地竞争，幻觉是盆地缺失。通过几何间隔（隐状态到最近盆地的距离）可有效区分正确召回与幻觉，优于输出熵方法，且该几何结构是模型固有的而非微调产物。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型的两种失败模式（参记忆与上下文信息冲突、从未学到事实的幻觉）均产生自信输出，基于输出的监控方法本质无效，需要从内部表征层面理解其机制。

Method: 通过合成任务（实体标识符映射到唯一编码，PM通过LoRA注入）验证几何解释；在自然语言事实查询上验证几何间隔优于输出熵的区分能力；分析幻觉比例随模型规模变化的标度律。

Result: 几何间隔可干净分离正确召回与幻觉，零误拒率；输出熵方法需拒绝绝大多数正确输出；幻觉比例服从标度律C=exp(-c/Δ̄)；隐状态可靠编码认知状态，但冻结的输出头系统性地擦除该信息，且随规模增大而恶化。

Conclusion: 几何间隔相比输出熵是更可靠的置信度指标；输出头擦除认知状态是幻觉检测困难的根本原因，且随规模放大。

Abstract: Language models draw on two knowledge sources: facts baked into weights (parametric memory, PM) and information in context (working memory, WM). We study two mechanistically distinct failure modes--conflict, when PM and WM disagree and interfere; and hallucination, when the queried fact was never learned. Both produce confident output regardless, making output-based monitoring blind by design. We show both failures share a unified geometric account. In the hidden-state space of autoregressive generation, learned facts form attractor basins. Conflict is basin competition: WM disrupts convergence to the correct basin without raising output entropy. Hallucination is basin absence: the hidden state drifts freely when no memorized basin exists. The frozen LM head, designed for next-token prediction, cannot distinguish these cases and fires confidently either way. We verify this account in a controlled synthetic task--entity identifiers mapped to unique codes with PM installed via LoRA adapters--where ground truth is exact and component roles can be causally isolated through targeted adapter placement. Geometric margin--the hidden state's distance to the nearest memorized basin--reads this geometry directly and separates correct recall from hallucination far more cleanly than output entropy, with zero false refusals where entropy-based detection cannot avoid rejecting the vast majority of correct outputs. The separation holds on natural-language factual queries from the pretrained model with no adaptation, confirming attractor geometry is structural rather than a fine-tuning artifact. The fraction of confident hallucinations follows a scaling law $C = \exp(-c/\barΔ)$, growing with scale even as overall error rates fall. Hidden states reliably encode epistemic state; the frozen output head systematically erases it--and this erasure worsens with scale.

</details>


### [184] [Proactive Instance Navigation with Comparative Judgment for Ambiguous User Queries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06223)
*Junhyuk Kwon,Seungjoon Lee,Hyejin Park,Kyle Min,Jungseul Ok*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出ProCompNav框架，通过候选池构建与比较判断二阶段策略，主动询问区分性二元问题来缩小候选集，在实例导航中提升成功率并减少用户响应长度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有实例导航方法在初始请求不明确时，要么过早停止于首个合理候选，要么基于单个候选询问属性而非区分性问题，导致无法有效区分目标与干扰项，增加用户负担。

Method: 两阶段框架：1) 构建候选池；2) 每轮提取属性-值对拆分当前池，提出二元是否问题，剪枝所有不一致候选，将开放目标描述转变为池级区分性提问。

Result: 在CoIN-Bench上，ProCompNav在相同最小输入下优于交互式基线，在详细描述下优于非交互式基线，并大幅减少响应长度；在TextNav上达到SOTA。

Conclusion: 比较判断策略对实例级导航中区分相似干扰项具有广泛适用性，能主动获取必要信息而非要求用户提供完整描述。

Abstract: Natural-language instance navigation becomes challenging when the initial user request does not uniquely specify the target instance. A practical agent should reduce the user's burden by actively asking only the information needed to distinguish the target from similar distractors, rather than requiring a detailed description upfront. Existing approaches often fall short of this goal: they may stop at the first plausible candidate before sufficiently exploring alternatives, or, even after collecting multiple candidates, ask about the target's attributes derived from individual candidates rather than questions selected to distinguish candidates in the pool. As a result, despite the dialogue, the agent may still fail to distinguish the target from distractors, leading to premature decisions and lengthy user responses. We propose Proactive Instance Navigation with Comparative Judgment (ProCompNav), a two-stage framework that first constructs a candidate pool and then identifies the target through comparative judgment. At each round, ProCompNav extracts an attribute-value pair that splits the current pool, asks a binary yes/no question, and prunes all inconsistent candidates at once. This reframes disambiguation from open-ended target description to pool-level discriminative questioning, where each question is chosen to narrow the candidate set. On CoIN-Bench, ProCompNav improves Success Rate over interactive baselines with the same minimal input and non-interactive baselines with detailed descriptions, while substantially reducing Response Length. ProCompNav also achieves state-of-the-art Success Rate on TextNav, suggesting that comparative judgment is broadly useful for instance-level navigation among similar distractors.

</details>


### [185] [DataDignity: Training Data Attribution for Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05687)
*Xiaomin Li,Andrzej Banburski-Fahey,Jaron Lanier*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出FakeWiki基准测试集（含3,537篇虚构维基文章）以及两种方法：无训练的激活融合检索方法SteerFuse和有监督对比模型ScoringModel。实验表明ScoringModel在Recall@10上比最强基线提升17.2个百分点（35.0→52.2），在对抗性查询条件下优势更明显。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有模型输出审计方法仅关注回答正确性，缺乏对"哪个源文档最支持该回答"的精确溯源能力。需要一种能区分真正答案支持与主题/词汇相似性的评估框架。

Method: 1) 构建FakeWiki基准：含3,537篇虚构文章，保留真实标签同时削弱词汇捷径；2) SteerFuse：无需训练的激活空间融合检索方法；3) ScoringModel：有监督对比学习模型，将回答与文档映射到共享空间，使用InfoNCE损失并结合批次内负样本、检索挖掘负样本和反文档负样本训练。

Result: 在9个开源LLM和5种查询条件下，ScoringModel的Recall@10从基线35.0提升至52.2，在41/45个模型-条件组合中最佳。在越狱攻击变体查询下，Recall@10平均提升15.7个百分点。

Conclusion: 可靠的数据归属需要能够区分真正答案支持与主题/词汇相似性的评估设置。激活空间证据可以高效补充文本检索。

Abstract: Auditing language-model outputs often requires more than judging correctness: an auditor may need to identify which source document most likely supports the knowledge expressed in a response. We study this as pinpoint provenance: given a prompt, a target-model response, and a candidate corpus, rank the documents that best support the response. We introduce FakeWiki, a controlled benchmark of 3,537 fabricated Wikipedia-style articles designed to preserve ground-truth provenance while weakening lexical shortcuts. FakeWiki includes QA probes, source-preserving paraphrases, retro-generated variants, hard anti-documents that remain topically similar while removing answer-critical facts, and five query conditions: clean prompting plus four jailbreak-inspired transformations. We evaluate seven retrieval baselines, a training-free activation-steering retrieval-fusion method, SteerFuse, and a supervised contrastive provenance ranker, ScoringModel. ScoringModel maps response and document features into a shared space and is trained with InfoNCE using in-batch, retrieval-mined, and anti-document negatives. Across nine open-weight instruction-tuned LLMs and five query conditions, ScoringModel improves mean Recall@10 from 35.0 for the strongest retrieval baseline to 52.2, without inference-time fusion, and wins 41/45 model-by-condition cells. SteerFuse is usually second-best despite requiring no supervised training, showing that activation-space evidence can efficiently complement text retrieval. On jailbreak-inspired transformed queries, ScoringModel improves Recall@10 by 15.7 points on average over the best baseline. Overall, our work shows that robust training data attribution requires evaluation settings that separate true answer support from topical or lexical resemblance.

</details>


### [186] [GCCM: Enhancing Generative Graph Prediction via Contrastive Consistency Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05689)
*Shaozhen Ma,Wei Huang,Hanchen Wang,Dong Wen,Wenjie Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出GCCM，通过对比一致性目标和特征扰动，解决图一致性训练中的捷径解问题，使扩散模型在保持稳定采样的同时超越确定性预测器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有扩散图预测方法推理时需昂贵迭代去噪且采样不稳定；一致性训练虽能加速但存在捷径解——模型忽略噪声目标退化为确定性预测器。

Method: 提出GCCM：(1) 引入负样本对构建对比一致性目标，增加分离约束使捷径解不再成立；(2) 对输入图节点/边特征加扰动，打破条件同质性，防止不同噪声层级输出相同预测。

Result: 在基准数据集上，GCCM有效缓解了捷径解问题，相较于确定性预测器获得一致的性能提升。

Conclusion: 对比一致性学习结合特征扰动是克服图扩散模型捷径解的有效策略，为稳定高效的图预测提供了新范式。

Abstract: Conditional generative models, particularly diffusion-based methods, have recently been applied to graph prediction by modeling the target as a conditional distribution given the input graph, yielding competitive results compared to deterministic predictor. However, existing diffusion-based prediction methods typically require expensive iterative denoising at inference and often suffer from unstable sampling, which motivates recent efforts to reduce inference denoising steps and enable stable sampling via techniques such as consistency training. Despite this progress, we find that existing consistency training methods for graph prediction could potentially fall into a shortcut solution: the model may attempt to satisfy the self-consistency constraint by ignoring the noisy target (i.e., assigning it negligible weight), ultimately collapsing into a purely deterministic predictor. To mitigate such shortcut solution, we propose GCCM, a graph contrastive consistency model that goes beyond isolated pairwise matching between the same target at different noise levels by introducing negative pairs into a contrastive consistency objective. This adds an additional separation requirement, making the shortcut solution no longer trivially sufficient to satisfy the proposed objective. Moreover, we apply feature perturbation to the input node/edge features to break identical conditioning on the input graph, so that the shortcut no longer yields the same predictions across noise levels and becomes less attractive. Extensive experiments on benchmark datasets demonstrate that GCCM mitigates the shortcut solution and yields consistent performance improvements in graph prediction compared to deterministic predictors.

</details>


### [187] [Saliency-Aware Regularized Quantization Calibration for Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05693)
*Yanlong Zhao,Xiaoyuan Cheng,Huihang Liu,Baihua He,Xinyu Zhang,Harrison Bo Hua Zhu,Wenlong Chen,Li Zeng,Zhuo Sun*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出SARQC，通过显著性感知正则化项改进后训练量化，使量化权重接近原始权重，降低泛化风险，提升大语言模型在困惑度和零样本准确率上的表现，且不增加推理计算开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有PTQ方法仅基于有限或代表性不足的校准数据最小化层间重构误差，可能导致量化权重偏离原始权重，使泛化风险发散，损害下游性能。

Method: 提出SARQC框架，在标准PTQ目标函数中加入显著性感知正则化项，鼓励量化权重在校准过程中保持接近原始权重，可无缝集成到现有PTQ流程中。

Result: 在密集型和混合专家大语言模型上，SARQC在困惑度和零样本准确率上均取得一致改进，且不增加推理计算开销。

Conclusion: SARQC通过统一框架增强了scale search和Gram-based方法，有效解决了PTQ泛化风险问题，是一种高效且实用的量化校准方案。

Abstract: Post-training quantization (PTQ) is an effective approach for deploying large language models (LLMs) under memory and latency constraints. Most existing PTQ methods determine quantization parameters by minimizing a layer-wise reconstruction error on a predetermined calibration dataset, usually optimized via either scale search or Gram-based methods. However, from the perspective of generalization risk, existing calibration objectives of PTQ based only on empirical reconstruction error on limited or unrepresentative calibration data could move the quantized weights away from the original weights. This may cause the generalization risk to diverge, potentially degrading downstream performance. To address this issue, we propose \emph{Saliency-Aware Regularized Quantization Calibration} (SARQC) a unified framework that augments the standard PTQ objective with a saliency-aware regularization term. This term encourages quantized weights to stay close to the original weights during calibration, leading to improved generalization during inference. SARQC integrates seamlessly into existing PTQ pipelines, enhancing both scale search and Gram-based methods under a unified formulation. Extensive experiments on dense and Mixture-of-Experts LLMs demonstrate consistent improvements in perplexity and zero-shot accuracy, without additional computational overhead during inference.

</details>


### [188] [Inference-Time Budget Control for LLM Search Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05701)
*Zhengru Fang,Senkang Forest Hu,Zhonghao Chang,Yu Guo,Yihang Tao,Hongyao Liu,Mengzhe Ruan,Jun Huang,Yuguang Fang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出双预算（工具调用次数+生成token数）受限下LLM搜索智能体的推理时预算控制方法，通过基于信息价值（VOI）的搜索动作选择和选择性答案修正，在多跳QA任务上超越多个基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM搜索智能体在推理时受工具调用次数和生成token数的双重硬约束，现有方法缺乏对"下一步该执行什么动作"以及"何时该提交最终答案"的显式控制，导致预算利用效率低下。

Method: 将问题形式化为两阶段推理时预算控制：1）搜索阶段，控制器为每个可行动作计算任务级信息价值（VOI）分数，即边际任务价值/单位预算的估计，据此选择检索、分解或答案提交；2）答案阶段，选择性证据接地终化器比较轨迹答案与优化候选，仅在残余错误为低风险答案格式错误时重写。

Result: 在4个多跳QA数据集、3种LLM骨干、4种预算水平下，该方法在相同双预算协议下一致优于4个基线。消融实验表明，搜索时预算控制（尤其是预算依赖惩罚）是主要性能来源，答案时控制仅在检索路径充分时有效。

Conclusion: LLM搜索智能体的推理时预算控制应同时管理搜索过程中的预算分配和最终答案的提交时机，双阶段控制比单一阶段控制更有效。

Abstract: LLM search agents increasingly rely on tools at inference time, but their trajectories are often constrained by hard limits on both tool calls and generated tokens. Under such dual budgets, better answers require not only stronger models, but also explicit control over which search action should receive the next budget unit and when the accumulated evidence is sufficient to commit a final answer. We study this problem in multi-hop question answering (QA) and formulate it as two-stage inference-time budget control. At search time, our controller assigns each feasible action a task-level Value-of-Information (VOI) score, defined as an operational estimate of marginal task value per unit budget under the current search state and remaining dual budget, and uses this score to choose among retrieval, decomposition, and answer commitment. After search, a selective evidence-grounded finalizer compares the trajectory answer with a refined candidate and rewrites only when the residual error appears to be a low-risk answer-form error. Across four multi-hop QA benchmarks, three LLM backbones, and four budget levels, the method yields positive aggregate gains over four audited baselines under the same hard dual-budget protocol. Ablations show that search-time budget control, especially budget-dependent penalty, provides the main performance gain, while answer-time control helps mainly when the retrieval path is already adequate. These results suggest that inference-time budget control for LLM search agents should govern both how budget is spent during search and how the final answer is committed.

</details>


### [189] [SkillRet: A Large-Scale Benchmark for Skill Retrieval in LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05726)
*Hongcheol Cho,Ryangkyung Kang,Youngeun Kim*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了SkillRet，一个大规模LLM Agent技能检索基准，包含17,810个技能和63,259个训练样本。实验发现现有检索器表现不佳，任务微调后NDCG@10提升13.1-16.9点，表明该领域仍有很大改进空间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM Agent技能库日益庞大，现有检索方法在小规模下可行，但在大规模、上下文和延迟受限的真实场景中表现不佳，且缺乏相关基准和深入研究。

Method: 构建SkillRet基准：收集17,810个公开Agent技能，设计结构化语义标签和两级分类体系（6大类、18子类），生成63,259训练样本和4,997评估查询。评测多种检索器并进行任务微调。

Result: 现有检索模型在大规模技能库上表现不佳；任务微调后NDCG@10相比最强先验检索器提升13.1点，相比最强现成检索器提升16.9点。性能提升源于微调模型能更好聚焦长噪声查询中的少量技能相关信号。

Conclusion: SkillRet可作为大规模Agent系统中技能检索研究的强基准和基础；当前技能检索问题远未解决，任务微调可带来显著提升。

Abstract: As LLM agents are increasingly deployed with large libraries of reusable skills, selecting the right skill for a user request has become a critical systems challenge. In small libraries, users may invoke skills explicitly by name, but this assumption breaks down as skill ecosystems grow under tight context and latency budgets. Despite its practical importance, skill retrieval remains underexplored, with limited benchmarks and little understanding of retrieval behavior on realistic skill libraries. To address this gap, we introduce SkillRet, a large-scale benchmark for skill retrieval in LLM agents. SkillRet contains 17,810 public agent skills, organized with structured semantic tags and a two-level taxonomy spanning 6 major categories and 18 sub-categories. It provides 63,259 training samples and 4,997 evaluation queries with disjoint skill pools, enabling both benchmarking and retrieval-oriented training. Across a diverse set of retrievers, we find that skill retrieval remains far from solved: off-the-shelf models struggle on realistic large-scale skill libraries, and prior skill-retrieval models still leave substantial headroom. Task-specific fine-tuning on SkillRet substantially improves performance, improving NDCG@10 by +13.1 points over the strongest prior retriever and by +16.9 points over the strongest off-the-shelf retriever. Our analysis further suggests that these gains arise because fine-tuned models better focus on the small skill-relevant signals within long and noisy queries. These results establish SkillRet as a strong benchmark and foundation for future research on retrieval in large-scale agent systems.

</details>


### [190] [Knee Osteoarthritis Severity Grading Using Optimized Deep Learning and LLM-Driven Intelligent AI on Computationally Limited Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05731)
*Dayam Nadeem,Neha,Safdar Mustafa,Adnan Alvi,Mohd Hussain*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结合ResNet-18 CNN与TensorFlow Lite的膝骨关节炎严重程度自动分级方法，通过迁移学习实现K-L分级，测试准确率达94.48%，并利用Gemini-2.0-flash大语言模型生成结构化解释性诊断报告，适用于资源受限和无网络环境。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统膝骨关节炎评估方法存在主观性强、观察者间变异大等问题，影响诊断准确性和及时性。因此，需要一种精确、自动化的诊断方法来评估KOA严重程度。

Method: 基于ResNet-18卷积神经网络，采用迁移学习对膝关节图像进行K-L五级分类；模型优化后转换为轻量级TensorFlow Lite格式，可在资源受限设备上离线运行；并集成Gemini-2.0-flash大语言模型生成症状、风险因素等结构化解释性结果。

Result: 训练过程中模型测试准确率达94.48%，收敛稳定；模型成功转换为TensorFlow Lite格式，实现无网络环境下的设备端部署。

Conclusion: 该研究验证了将深度学习与大语言模型结合、构建可部署于资源受限设备的可解释性决策支持工具的可行性，有助于提升AI辅助膝筛查工具的可及性和早期诊断能力。

Abstract: Knee osteoarthritis (KOA) is among the musculoskeletal disorders that considerably restrict joint mobility, cause severe chronic pain and impact negatively on quality life. It is one of the persistent health issues worldwide. Generally, subjectivity and inter-observer variability undermine conventional practices and evaluation process that are adopted to address such health issues. Hence precise and timely diagnosis would be one of the effective ways for the assessment of its severity. This paper proposes an automated diagnostic approach for severity grading of KOA by blending a deep learning convolutional neural network (CNN) with a device-based inference platform powered by TensorFlow Lite. It proposes a model based on the ResNet-18 convolutional neural network. The designed model is trained on publicly available database. Through a transfer learning approach obtained knee images are first classified into five Kellgren-Lawrence (KL) grades. Further the developed model is optimised. During the training of the model test accuracy of 94.48% with stable convergence has been achieved. Subsequently the optimised model transformed into a lightweight TensorFlow Lite format, facilitating seamless deployment on resource-constrained devices. The designed model is capable enough to operate in the environment having no continuous internet connectivity. Also, an auxiliary Large Language Model (Gemini-2.0-flash) is applied to generate structured interpretive findings like potential symptoms, risk factors, and preventive majors etc. The LLM component functions as interface without influencing the classification process. The proposed model articulates the feasibility of an on-device, interpretable decision-support tools for early diagnosis and improve accessibility to Artificial Intelligence (AI)-assisted knee screening tool.

</details>


### [191] [SDFlow: Similarity-Driven Flow Matching for Time Series Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05736)
*Wei Li,Shibo Feng,Pengcheng Wu,Min Wu,Peilin Zhao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SDFlow是一个非自回归的流匹配框架，在冻结的VQ潜空间中并行生成时间序列，通过解决曝光偏差、高维VQ空间和离散监督融合三大挑战，在长序列生成中取得SOTA性能并显著提升推理速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的VQ+自回归范式在时间序列生成中存在曝光偏差问题，即推理时错误会逐步累积，导致长序列生成质量严重下降。

Method: 提出SDFlow框架：(1) 用全局传输图替代逐步token预测消除曝光偏差；(2) 通过低秩流形分解和学习锚点先验降低VQ空间维度；(3) 引入类别后验分布将离散监督融入连续流匹配动力学。

Result: 在Discriminative Score和Context-FID上达到SOTA，尤其在长序列生成中效果显著，同时推理速度大幅快于自回归基线。

Conclusion: SDFlow在保持高保真度的同时实现了计算效率的提升，为时间序列生成提供了一种兼具质量和速度的非自回归替代方案。

Abstract: Vector quantization (VQ) with autoregressive (AR) token modeling is a widely adopted and highly competitive paradigm for time-series generation. However, such models are fundamentally limited by exposure bias: during inference, errors can accumulate across sequential predictions, leading to pronounced quality degradation in long-horizon generation. To address this, we propose SDFlow ($\textbf{S}$imilarity-$\textbf{D}$riven $\textbf{Flow}$ Matching), a non-autoregressive framework that operates entirely in the frozen VQ latent space and enables parallel sequence generation via flow matching. We tackle three key challenges in making this transition: (1) eliminating exposure bias by replacing step-wise token prediction with a global transport map; (2) mitigating the high-dimensionality of VQ token spaces via a low-rank manifold decomposition with a learned anchor prior over the latent manifold; and (3) incorporating discrete supervision into continuous transport dynamics by introducing a categorical posterior over codebook indices within a variational flow-matching formulation. Extensive experiments show that SDFlow achieves state-of-the-art performance, improving Discriminative Score and substantially reducing Context-FID, particularly for challenging long-sequence generation. Moreover, SDFlow provides significant inference speedups over autoregressive baselines, offering both high fidelity and computational efficiency. Code is available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/SDFlow-D6F3/

</details>


### [192] [ReFlect: An Effective Harness System for Complex Long-Horizon LLM Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05737)
*Fan Huang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出ReFlect，一个模型无关、无需训练、纯推理时使用的LLM推理增强框架。它通过确定性包装器实现独立的错误检测与恢复逻辑，解决了传统推理范式（如CoT、ReAct）在长程多阶段任务中错误累积的问题。实验表明，ReFlect在6个推理域上取得了显著提升，且增益与模型基线表现呈负相关（斜率-1.69）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM推理范式（CoT、ReAct、事后自我批评）依赖两个在长程多阶段任务中失效的假设，导致错误在推理步骤中无声累积。核心问题是：推理系统能否有效检测并恢复自身错误？

Method: 提出ReFlect框架，作为LLM推理的"缰绳"系统，创建独立的错误检测和恢复逻辑，以确定性包装器（deterministic wrapper）的形式围绕模型运行，不依赖模型自身的自我批评能力。

Result: 1) 自我批评方法在90/100的审核块中未发现问题，模型在≥76%情况下错误接受错误答案。2) ReFlect任务成功率：GPT-4o-mini 41%至Claude Sonnet 4.5 56%，相对于Direct CoT提升+7pp至+29pp。3) SWE-bench补丁结构质量从0%提升至82%-87%。4) 增益与基线成功率呈负相关（斜率-1.69，r=-0.76）。5) 添加结构化推理状态在Llama-3.3-70B和Qwen2.5-72B上仅15-18.7%，因模型无法可靠填充状态。

Conclusion: ReFlect是一种有效的、模型无关的推理增强方法，特别对弱基线模型提升显著。结构化推理状态对中等规模模型效果有限。研究揭示了自我批评范式的根本局限，并提出了一种可替代的确定性方案。

Abstract: Current reasoning paradigms for LLMs include chain-of-thought, ReAct, and post-hoc self-critique. These paradigms rely on two assumptions that fail on long-horizon, multi-stage tasks. As a result, errors accumulate silently across reasoning steps, leaving an open question: can a reasoning system effectively detect and recover from its own failures? We present ReFlect, a \emph{harness} system for LLM reasoning that creates standalone error detection and recovery logic as a deterministic wrapper around the model. Controlled experiments across 6 reasoning domains show that prompt-level self-critique produces formulaic templates that flag no issues in 90 of 100 audited reflection blocks, and the investigated LLMs wrongly accept a wrong answer in at least 76\% of cases. Our ReFlect harness achieves task success rates ranging from 41\% on gpt-4o-mini to 56\% on Claude Sonnet 4.5 across six models spanning small and frontier scale, with per-model gains over Direct CoT ranging from +7 pp on Qwen2.5-72B to +29 pp on Claude Sonnet 4.5, and additionally raises SWE-bench patch-structural quality from 0\% (Direct CoT) to between 82\% (Qwen2.5-72B) and 87\% (GPT-4o). Notably, the harness gain is inversely proportional to the model's Direct CoT task success rate (the fitted slope is -1.69 with r=-0.76): each pp lost in baseline success rate is mechanically recovered by 1.69 pp of harness gain. We spot that adding structured reasoning state and operators yields only 15.0--18.7\% pair-mean on Llama-3.3-70B and Qwen2.5-72B because models at this scale cannot reliably populate the state its operators require. ReFlect is model-agnostic, training-free, and operates entirely at inference time.

</details>


### [193] [HyperLens: Quantifying Cognitive Effort in LLMs with Fine-grained Confidence Trajectory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05741)
*Chengda Lu,Xiaoyu Fan,Wei Xu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文发现Transformer深层存在内在的置信度放大机制，基于此提出高分辨率探针HyperLens，通过追踪置信度轨迹量化推理过程中的认知努力，并揭示复杂任务需要更高认知努力，同时诊断了标准SFT会降低认知努力从而损害领域内任务性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有分析工具分辨率有限，难以理解LLM的推理动态。

Method: 发现Transformer深层对置信度微小变化有内在放大机制，提出高分辨率探针HyperLens，追踪置信度轨迹并量化认知努力。

Result: HyperLens揭示复杂任务与简单任务的置信度轨迹存在一致性差异，且复杂任务需要更高的认知努力；标准SFT会降低认知努力，损害领域内任务性能。

Conclusion: LLM推理中认知努力是区分任务复杂度的关键指标，且SFT的副作用可通过认知努力视角进行解释。

Abstract: While Large Language Models (LLMs) achieve strong performance across diverse tasks, their inference dynamics remain poorly understood because of the limited resolution of existing analysis tools. In this work, we identify an intrinsic magnification mechanism in transformer architectures: deeper layers inherently magnify the small changes of layer-wise confidence, providing a fine-grained confidence trajectory. Building on this insight, we introduce HyperLens, a high-resolution probe designed to trace confidence trajectories and quantify the cognitive effort during inference. Across LLMs and datasets, HyperLens reveals a consistent divergence in confidence trajectories that separates complex from simple tasks. We abstract this pattern into a quantitative cognitive effort metric. Our analysis reveals a fundamental principle: complex tasks consistently require higher cognitive effort. Finally, we provide a mechanistic diagnosis of a common side effect of standard Supervised Fine-Tuning (SFT): it can reduce cognitive effort and consequently degrade performance on in-domain tasks.

</details>


### [194] [Best Arm Identification in Generalized Linear Bandits via Hybrid Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05745)
*Qirun Zeng,Xuchuang Wang,Jiayi Shen,Xutong Liu,Fang Kong,Jinhang Zuo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了广义线性bandits中固定置信度的最佳臂识别问题，提出了一种混合反馈模型，该模型结合了单臂的绝对奖励反馈和臂对的相对（对抗）反馈，并基于似然比的置信序列设计了混合Track-and-Stop算法，在理论保证和实验中都取得了更优的样本效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在广义线性bandits的固定置信度最佳臂识别问题中，现有工作通常只考虑单一反馈类型（绝对奖励或相对反馈），而实际场景中两者可能同时可获得，且获取成本各异。因此，作者希望设计一种统一框架，允许学习者在绝对和相对反馈间灵活切换，并考虑成本差异，以提升样本效率和实用性。

Method: 1. 提出一种基于似然比的置信序列，统一处理异构的广义线性观测数据，并在自协调假设下得到显式的椭球置信集。2. 基于该置信集，提出混合Track-and-Stop算法，通过追踪联合动作空间（臂和臂对）上的极小极大最优设计，自适应分配查询。3. 进一步扩展至考虑不同反馈模态获取成本的成本感知设置。

Result: 1. 建立了算法的δ-正确性证明。2. 给出了停止时间的高概率上界。3. 实验表明，所提出算法相比基线方法显著提高了样本效率。

Conclusion: 本文提出的混合反馈框架和Track-and-Stop算法有效地统一了绝对和相对反馈下的最佳臂识别问题，不仅提供了理论保证，还在实验中展现了优越性能，成本感知的扩展进一步增强了其实用价值。

Abstract: We study fixed-confidence best arm identification in generalized linear bandits under a hybrid feedback model: at each round, the learner may query either (i) absolute reward feedback from a single arm or (ii) relative (dueling) feedback from an arm pair, both governed by generalized linear models. We introduce a likelihood-ratio--based confidence sequence that unifies heterogeneous generalized linear observations and yields an explicit ellipsoidal confidence set under a self-concordance assumption. Building on this confidence set, we propose a hybrid Track-and-Stop algorithm that adaptively allocates queries by tracking a minimax-optimal design over a joint action space of arms and pairs. We establish $δ$-correctness and provide high-probability upper bounds on the stopping time. We further extend the framework to a cost-aware setting that accounts for heterogeneous acquisition costs across feedback modalities. Empirical experiments demonstrate that the proposed algorithms significantly improve sample efficiency over baseline methods.

</details>


### [195] [Evaluating Explainability in Safety-Critical ATR Systems: Limitations of Post-Hoc Methods and Paths Toward Robust XAI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05748)
*Vanessa Buhrmester,David Muench,Dimitri Bulatov,Michael Arens*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文系统评估了可解释人工智能（XAI）方法在安全关键型自动目标识别（ATR）系统中的应用，指出现有事后解释方法存在虚假解释、扰动不稳定和过度信任等关键失效模式，并提出需要向更鲁棒、因果驱动和物理信息化的可解释性方法发展。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在安全关键的ATR系统中，仅靠高预测性能不足以保证系统可靠性，模型决策需要可解释、可信赖且可验证。现有XAI方法缺乏系统性的安全评估框架。

Method: 识别主要XAI范式（显著性、注意力、替代方法及检测感知扩展），形式化可解释性为保证导向的评估问题，构建分类法，并从可解释性、鲁棒性、操纵脆弱性、验证与确认适用性四个维度评估这些方法。

Result: 发现当前事后XAI方法存在系统性的局限性，包括虚假解释、扰动不稳定、视觉上令人信服但导致过度信任等关键失效模式。

Conclusion: 广泛使用的XAI技术可能不足以支撑安全关键型部署，需超越视觉上合理的解释，转向支持可靠决策和系统级保证的方法，如更鲁棒、因果驱动和物理信息化的可解释性方法。

Abstract: Explainable Artificial Intelligence (XAI) is increasingly rec ognized as essential for deploying machine learning systems in safety critical environments. In Automatic Target Recognition (ATR), where models operate on image, video, radar, and multisensor data, high pre dictive performance alone is insufficient. Model decisions must also be interpretable, reliable, and suitable for validation. This paper presents a structured evaluation of explainability methods in the context of safety-critical ATR systems: We identify major XAI paradigms, including saliency-based, attention-based, and surrogate ap proaches, as well as recent detection-aware extensions. Based on this, we formalize explainability as an assurance-oriented assessment problem, introduce a taxonomy, and assess these methods with respect to four key dimensions: interpretability, robustness, vulnerability to manipula tion, and suitability for validation and verification. The analysis identifies systematic limitations of current post-hoc explanation methods. In par ticular, we derive critical failure modes such as spurious explanations, instability under perturbations, and overtrust induced by visually con vincing outputs. These findings indicate that widely used XAI techniques may be insufficient for safety-critical deployment. Finally, we discuss implications for ATR systems and outline directions toward more robust, causally grounded, and physically informed explain ability methods. Our results emphasize the need to move beyond visually plausible explanations toward approaches that support reliable decision making and system-level assurance.

</details>


### [196] [Confidence is the key: how conformal prediction enhances the generative design of permeable peptides](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05770)
*Laura van Weesep,Sunay Chankeshwara,Leonardo De Maria,Florian David,Ola Engkvist,Gökçe Geylan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结合强化学习（RL）与共形预测（CP）的生成框架，用于设计具有膜渗透性的环肽分子。通过CP量化预测不确定性，避免RL探索超出预测模型适用域的高不确定性化学空间，从而提升分子设计的可靠性和效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 环肽对细胞内靶点具有治疗潜力，但被动膜渗透性设计极具挑战；现有生成模型结合RL时，预测模型可能因超出适用域而产生不可靠预测，导致设计陷入高奖励但高不确定性的化学空间。

Method: 基于RL的生成框架（类似REINVENT/PepINVENT），使用不确定性感知的渗透性预测器作为评分组件；引入共形预测（CP）量化预测不确定性，在用户定义的置信水平下校准模型，奖励CP-informed预测的肽序列，抑制超出适用域的探索。

Result: CP-informed奖励机制显著提升了肽优化过程的可靠性和效率，有效减少了模型在预测器适用域外的探索行为。

Conclusion: 首次将生成建模与共形预测相结合，弥合了预测不确定性与RL引导探索之间的鸿沟，为未来将不确定性量化融入分子设计提供了新范式。

Abstract: Generative models coupled with reinforcement learning (RL), such as REINVENT and PepINVENT, have emerged as a powerful framework for de novo molecular design. During the ideation process these generative frameworks utilize various predictive models as part of the optimization objectives. However, the utility of the predictive models can be limited by their domain of applicability. When RL is used to explore the chemical space with predictive models, it can suggest molecules that lie outside the predictor's domain of applicability. As a result, the predictions may become less reliable, potentially steering designs into high reward but also high uncertainty chemical spaces. This is particularly pronounced for cyclic peptides which show therapeutic promise due to their modifiability and large interaction surfaces but are understudied compared to small molecules. While passive membrane permeation in cyclic peptides has attracted interest, identifying optimal permeable designs remains challenging yet crucial for targeting intracellular sites. We present an RL-guided generative framework that designs permeable cyclic peptides using an uncertainty-aware permeability predictor as the scoring component. To address predictive uncertainty, especially impacted by novel chemistry, we integrate conformal prediction (CP) as our uncertainty quantification method. CP assesses designs based on the calibrated model under a user-defined confidence level. We demonstrate that rewarding generated peptides with CP-informed predictions improves both reliability and efficiency of peptide optimization process. This also discourages exploration outside the predictor's applicability domain. This approach bridges the gap between predictive uncertainty and RL-guided exploration, showing how generative modelling and conformal prediction can be combined for the first time.

</details>


### [197] [CircuitFormer: A Circuit Language Model for Analog Topology Design from Natural Language Prompt](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05773)
*Md Touhidul Islam,Sujan Kumar Saha,Farimah Farahmandi,Mark Tehranipoor*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文针对模拟电路自动化设计挑战，构建了包含31,341对网表-自然语言描述的最大规模标注数据集，提出新型电路图分词器CKT（实现O(1)词汇量复杂度），并训练了511M参数的电路专用语言模型CircuitFormer，在语法正确率（100%）和功能成功率（83%）上显著超越现有开源LLM，参数量减少240倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Transformer-based LLMs在模拟硬件设计中的应用受限于两个关键瓶颈：（i）模拟电路设计领域缺少包含自然语言描述与对应网表的标注数据集；（ii）通用分词器（如BPE）无法有效捕捉电路固有的图结构信息。

Method: （1）构建最大规模模拟电路网表-自然语言描述数据集（31,341对）；（2）提出Circuit Tokenizer (CKT)，通过挖掘高频子电路来编码网表连接性，将词汇量复杂度从O(n_max)降至O(1)；（3）基于CKT优化分词，训练511M参数的编码器-解码器Transformer模型CircuitFormer。

Result: CKT相比BPE将序列长度减少57%，压缩比提升2.3倍（固定词汇量512）。CircuitFormer实现100%语法正确率和83%功能成功率，优于SOTA开源LLM分别达10%和14%，且参数量减少240倍。

Conclusion: 本文证明了专门针对电路图结构设计的分词器和领域专用语言模型能有效克服通用LLM在模拟电路设计中的局限性，为EDA自动化提供了新范式。

Abstract: Automating analog circuit design remains a longstanding challenge in Electronic Design Automation (EDA). While Transformer-based Large Language Models (LLMs) have revolutionized software code generation, their application to analog hardware design is hindered by two critical limitations: (i) the scarcity of analog design datasets containing natural language description of a design and its corresponding netlist, and (ii) the inefficiency of general-purpose tokenizers (e.g., Byte Pair Encoding (BPE)) in capturing the inherent graph structure of circuits. To bridge this gap, first, we curate the largest annotated dataset of analog circuit netlists to date, comprising 31,341 netlist-natural language description pairs across all major circuit classes. Furthermore, we propose Circuit Tokenizer (CKT), a novel circuit graph tokenizer designed to encode netlist connectivity by explicitly mining frequent subcircuits. In terms of scalability, CKT overcomes the bottleneck of prior circuit graph serialization methods where vocabulary size scales linearly with maximum number of components in the dataset, n_max, (O(n_max)); instead, CKT decouples vocabulary growth from circuit complexity, achieving a constant O(1) complexity. Empirically, CKT outperforms standard BPE on circuit topology representation, reducing sequence length by 57% and achieving a 2.3x superior compression ratio using a compact, fixed vocabulary of size 512. Leveraging this optimized tokenization, we train a circuit-specific language model, CircuitFormer, a 511M parameter encoder-decoder transformer. Our model achieves 100% syntactic correctness and an 83% functional success rate across all major analog circuit categories, outperforming state-of-the-art open-source LLMs by 10% and 14%, respectively, while requiring 240x fewer parameters. The dataset is publicly available at https://huggingface.co/datasets/touhid314/cktformer-dataset.

</details>


### [198] [HEDP: A Hybrid Energy-Distance Prompt-based Framework for Domain Incremental Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05776)
*Yu Feng,Zhen Tian,Haoran Luo,Xie Yu,Diancheng Cheng,Haoyue Zheng,Shuai Lyu,Ping Zong,Lianyuan Li,Xin Ge,Yifan Zhu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出HEDP框架，基于Helmholtz自由能，通过能量正则化损失和混合能量-距离加权机制，在域增量学习中提升未见域的泛化性能，在CORe50等基准上取得2.57%的准确率提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 域增量学习中，域偏移会导致模型性能严重下降，现有方法在缓解灾难性遗忘和提升开放世界适应性方面仍有不足。

Method: 提出Hybrid Energy-Distance Prompt (HEDP) 框架：1) 引入能量正则化损失增强域表示的可分离性；2) 设计混合能量-距离加权机制，融合基于能量和基于距离的信号，以改进域选择与泛化能力。

Result: 在CORe50等多个基准上，HEDP在未见域上取得2.57%的准确率提升，有效缓解了灾难性遗忘，增强了开放世界适应性。

Conclusion: HEDP通过能量视角的域表示学习与加权机制，为域增量学习提供了一种有效且可泛化的解决方案。

Abstract: Domain Incremental Learning is a critical scenario that requires models to continuously adapt to new data domains without retraining. However, domain shifts often cause severe performance degradation. To address this, we propose Hybrid Energy-Distance Prompt, a domain-incremental framework inspired by Helmholtz free energy. HEDP introduces an energy regularization loss to enhance the separability of domain representations and a hybrid energy-distance weighted mechanism that fuses energy-based and distance-based cues to improve domain selection and generalization. Experiments on multiple benchmarks, including CORe50, show that HEDP achieves superior performance on unseen domains with a 2.57\% accuracy gain, effectively mitigating catastrophic forgetting and enhancing open-world adaptability. Our code is \href{https://github.com/dannis97500/HEDP/}{available here}.

</details>


### [199] [Von Neumann Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05780)
*Shekhar S. Chandra*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文基于冯·诺依曼的细胞阵列计算系统，提出了“冯·诺依曼神经元”及其构成的“冯·诺依曼网络”(VNNs)，这是一种架构仅由输入输出位置决定的自工程设计神经网络。VNNs基于神经算子与格林函数学习，在细胞拓扑上具有扩散特征，被证明是通用计算系统。实验表明，VNN多层感知机在基础任务上优于同等深度学习变体，参数效率更高，并能学习新类型任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将冯·诺依曼在20世纪中期提出的细胞阵列计算模型（最初作为人脑的简化模型）与现代深度学习相结合，构建一种架构自设计、参数高效的神经网络。

Method: 提出"冯·诺依曼神经元"概念，每个细胞具有可学习的专业角色；构建"冯·诺依曼网络"(VNNs)，其架构仅依赖于输入输出在细胞阵列上的结构和位置；数学框架基于神经算子的扩展和格林函数的学习，使用具有扩散特征的细胞拓扑上的卷积操作。

Result: VNN多层感知机在基础任务上优于同等深度学习变体，参数效率更高，能学习新类型任务，并能为所有现代计算机通用的冯·诺依曼（硬件）架构构建细胞层面的扩展。

Conclusion: VNNs属于更通用的"细胞机器"计算系统，被证明具有计算通用性，为自设计神经网络架构和新型计算范式开辟了新可能性。

Abstract: In the mid-twentieth century, mathematician and polymath John von Neumann created a computational system on an array of cells as a simple model of the human brain, where each cell had one of a finite set of roles or states that he predicted would be modelled by a diffusion process. In this work, we show that such a system, when developed in a modern deep learning setting, enables the construction of an artificial neuron having specialized roles that can be learnt. We refer to this neuron as the Von Neumann neuron, and the resulting neural network from such neurons result in a self-engineered design whose architecture is only dependent on the structure and locations of its inputs and outputs on this cellular array. The mathematical framework for these Von Neumann Networks (VNNs) is also constructed and shows that they are based on the extension of neural operators and the learning of Green's functions with convolutions on a cellular topology having a diffusion signature. We also prove that these VNNs are part of a more general computational system called Cellular Machines that are computationally universal. Initial experiments show that VNN based multi-layered perceptrons outperform their equivalent deep learning variant on basic tasks, while being more parameter efficient and are capable of learning new types of tasks. This includes the ability to solve for and construct an extension of the Von Neumann (hardware) architecture common to all modern computers to cells and suggests new opportunities that could be explored.

</details>


### [200] [Sheet as Token: A Graph-Enhanced Representation for Multi-Sheet Spreadsheet Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05811)
*Yiming Lei,Yiqi Wang,Yujia Zhang,Bo Guan,Depei Zhu,Chunhui Wang,Zhuonan Hao,Tianyu Shi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为"Sheet as Token"的图增强框架，将每个工作表视为一个语义单元（sheet token），通过图检索器构建查询相关的候选图，利用多阶段图Transformer进行跨表推理，实现多工作表规模的电子表格理解与检索。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于检索增强的方法将电子表格分解为行、列或块，这种以块为中心的表征方式会碎片化工作表，削弱全局的sheet级语义信息，导致跨表格信息检索困难。

Method: 1) 将每个工作表编码为紧凑的稠密token（提取sheet名称、列头、代表值、布局特征等模式感知记录）；2) 构建图检索器，基于语义、查询条件、模式一致性和形状兼容性关系构建候选图；3) 使用多阶段图Transformer组合这些关系通道，检索支持性sheet集合。

Result: 在构建的多工作表语料库上，sheet级token化能学习稳定表征，图增强的跨表推理在listwise检索上优于浅层图基线，且额外图侧计算开销有限。

Conclusion: Sheet级token化是一种有前景的可扩展多工作表理解抽象方法。

Abstract: Workbook-scale spreadsheet understanding is increasingly important for language-model-based data analysis agents, but remains challenging because relevant information is often distributed across multiple sheets with heterogeneous schemas, layouts, and implicit relationships. Existing retrieval-augmented approaches typically decompose spreadsheets into rows, columns, or blocks to improve scalability; however, such chunk-centric representations can fragment worksheets into isolated text spans and weaken global sheet-level semantics. We propose Sheet as Token, a graph-enhanced framework that treats each worksheet as a unified semantic unit for multi-sheet spreadsheet retrieval. Our method extracts schema-aware records from sheet names, column headers, representative values, and layout features, and encodes each worksheet into a compact dense token. Given a natural-language query, a Graph Retriever constructs a query-specific candidate graph over sheet tokens using semantic, query-conditioned, schema-consistency, and shape-compatibility relations, and composes these channels through a multi-stage graph transformer to retrieve supporting sheet sets. Experiments on a constructed multi-sheet spreadsheet corpus show that sheet-level tokenization learns stable representations, and that graph-enhanced cross-sheet reasoning improves listwise retrieval over a shallow graph baseline with limited additional graph-side computation. These results suggest that sheet-level tokenization is a promising abstraction for scalable multi-sheet spreadsheet understanding.

</details>


### [201] [Long-Horizon Q-Learning: Accurate Value Learning via n-Step Inequalities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05812)
*Armaan A. Abraham,Lucy Xiaoyang Shi,Chelsea Finn*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出长视界Q学习（LQL），通过最优性下界约束作为正则化项，在不增加额外网络或前向传播的前提下，有效抑制TD学习中的误差累积，在多种基准上持续优于1步和n步TD方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于价值的离线强化学习方法（如Q-learning）可以利用任意经验数据，但自举（bootstrapping）导致长视界学习不稳定：后期状态的估计误差通过时序差分更新反向传播并随时间累积。现有方法对此问题缺乏有效的机制性缓解手段。

Method: LQL利用最优性收紧不等式：任何已执行的行动序列在期望意义上是真实最优策略的下界，因此提前采取最优行为不应比先跟随观测行为若干步再切换为最优行为更差。LQL将此不等式转化为铰链损失（hinge loss），作为Q-learning的惩罚项来约束违反该界的行为。惩罚项的计算复用已有网络输出，无需辅助网络或额外前向传播。

Result: 在在线和离线到在线的多种基准上，LQL与多种SOTA方法结合，持续优于1步TD和n步TD学习，且运行时开销相似。

Conclusion: LQL提供了一种即插即用的稳定机制，在不增加计算成本的前提下有效缓解了长视界Q-learning中的误差累积问题，具有广泛的适用性。

Abstract: Off-policy, value-based reinforcement learning methods such as Q-learning are appealing because they can learn from arbitrary experience, including data collected by older policies or other agents. In practice, however, bootstrapping makes long-horizon learning brittle: estimation errors at later states propagate backward through temporal-difference (TD) updates and can compound over time. We propose long-horizon Q-learning (LQL), which introduces a principled backstop against compounding error when learning the optimal action-value function. LQL builds on a prior optimality tightening observation: any realized action sequence lower-bounds what the optimal policy can achieve in expectation, so acting optimally earlier should not be worse than following the observed actions for several steps before switching to optimal behavior. Our contribution is to turn this inequality into a practical stabilization mechanism for Q-learning by using a hinge loss to penalize violations of these bounds. Importantly, LQL computes these penalties using network outputs already produced for the TD error, requiring no auxiliary networks and no additional forward passes relative to Q-learning. When combined with multiple state-of-the-art methods on a range of online and offline-to-online benchmarks, LQL consistently outperforms both 1-step TD and n-step TD learning at similar runtime.

</details>


### [202] [AGPO: Asymmetric Group Policy Optimization for Verifiable Reasoning and Search Ads Relevance at JD](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05826)
*Yang Xu,Kun Yao,Yiming Deng,Zheng Fang,Kai Ming Ting,Ming Pang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出非对称群体策略优化（AGPO），通过负向主导强化策略抑制错误推理路径，结合群体优势机制放大稀有正确路径的更新，在保持基础模型探索能力的同时，在数学推理和搜索广告数据标注任务中取得SOTA性能，并有效提升大样本下的pass@k覆盖。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RLVR方法虽能提升正确路径的采样效率，但会导致模型推理能力边界收缩，基础模型在大量采样时反而有更高覆盖度。需要一种方法既能强化正确推理，又不抑制模型的探索能力。

Method: 提出非对称群体策略优化（AGPO）：1）负向强化采用负向主导策略，抑制错误推理路径；2）正向强化采用群体优势机制，根据组内方差缩放正向更新幅度，聚焦稀有正确路径，抑制平凡路径的更新。

Result: 在五个数学基准上达到SOTA准确率，且持续提升大样本下的pass@k性能；在大规模搜索广告相关性优化工业应用中，显著提升数据标注质量，进而提升下游学生模型性能。

Conclusion: AGPO通过非对称的正负强化策略，有效缓解了RLVR训练导致的推理边界收缩问题，在保持探索能力的同时提升推理性能，并在工业场景中验证了其实际价值。

Abstract: Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Rewards (RLVR) has demonstrated notable success in enhancing the reasoning performance of large language models (LLMs). However, recent studies reveal that while current RLVR methods improve sampling efficiency towards correct paths, they do not elicit fundamentally new reasoning patterns. Instead, the reasoning capability boundary of trained models often narrows compared to their base models, with base models achieving higher coverage at large sample sizes. In this work, we propose Asymmetric Group Policy Optimization (AGPO) to counteract this boundary shrinkage. AGPO adopts a negative-dominant reinforcement strategy to suppress incorrect reasoning paths, maintaining the base model's exploration capacity. For positive reinforcement, AGPO adopts a group advantage mechanism, which scales positive updates based on intra-group variance, allowing the model to focus on rare correct paths while suppressing updates from trivial paths. Our experiments on five mathematical benchmarks demonstrate that AGPO achieves state-of-the-art accuracy while consistently improving pass@$k$ performance at scale. In a large-scale industrial application for search ads relevance optimization, AGPO effectively enhances the quality of the data annotation, leading to substantial performance gains in downstream student models.

</details>


### [203] [MolRecBench-Wild: A Real-World Benchmark for Optical Chemical Structure Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05832)
*Haote Yang,Hui Wang,Chen Zhu,Jingchao Wang,Linye Li,Hongbin Lai,Huijie Ao,Yongxuan Lyu,Jiang Wu,Jiaxing Sun,Lua Chen,Yuanyuan Cao,Ruijie Zhang,Shengxin Lu,Lijun Wu,Bin Wang,Conghui He*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出MOSAIC框架（双维度37细粒度标签分类分子图视觉与化学难度）、构建MolRecBench-Wild基准（820篇论文5029个真实结构）、设计CARBON表示语言（支持价态变化、图标基团等非标准语义），并采用双轨评估协议。18个模型实验表明现有系统在真实学术场景下性能严重下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有OCSR系统在真实世界图像上不可靠，因为存在严重的视觉干扰和化学语义复杂性；同时缺乏能全面评估真实学术论文中分子图的标准化基准和语义表示方法。

Method: 提出MOSAIC双维度难度框架（37个细粒度标签），构建MolRecBench-Wild基准（820篇近期化学论文中的5029个结构），设计CARBON表示语言（支持价态变化、图标基团等非标准语义），并采用CARBON与SMILES双轨评估协议。

Result: 在18个OCSR模型上的综合实验表明，这些模型在MolRecBench-Wild上性能严重下降，揭示了先前专利基准与真实学术场景之间存在巨大差距。

Conclusion: 真实学术场景中分子图识别的难度远超现有专利基准，现有的OCSR系统尚不可靠，MOSAIC框架、MolRecBench-Wild基准和CARBON表示语言为该领域提供了更全面的评估工具和未来改进方向。

Abstract: Optical Chemical Structure Recognition (OCSR) aims to translate molecular diagrams in scientific literature into machine-readable formats, but current systems remain unreliable on real-world images due to substantial visual and chemical complexity. We introduce MOSAIC, a dual-dimensional difficulty framework with 37 fine-grained labels that jointly characterize visual interference and chemical semantic challenges in molecular diagrams. Based on this framework, we construct MolRecBench-Wild, a benchmark of 5,029 structures from 820 recent chemistry papers, covering the full difficulty spectrum observed in real publications. To enable faithful semantic evaluation beyond SMILES and MolFile, we propose CARBON, a representation language capable of expressing valence variations, icon-based groups, and other non-standard chemical semantics. We further adopt a dual-track evaluation protocol supporting both CARBON and SMILES outputs for broad model compatibility. Comprehensive experiments over 18 OCSR-capable models reveal severe performance degradation on MolRecBench-Wild, exposing a large gap between previous patent benchmarks and real-world academic scenarios.

</details>


### [204] [On the Role of Language Representations in Auto-Bidding: Findings and Implications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05833)
*Guanyu Zhu,Jining Luan,Hanwen Du,Xinyu Fang,Sibo Xu,Ersheng Ni,Hongji Li,Jincheng Fang,Ronghao Chen,Huacan Wang,Xuanqi Lan,Yongxin Ni,Yiqi Sun,Youhua Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出SemBid框架，通过在出价轨迹中注入LLM编码的语义Token（任务、历史、策略），利用自注意力机制融合语义与数值特征，提升了自动出价策略的可控性、泛化能力和约束满足能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动出价方法依赖紧凑数值状态表示，虽能隐式捕捉投放动态，但缺乏对高层意图、动态反馈和运营策略式指导的显式表示与控制能力。LLM虽能编码语义信息，但何时有用以及如何与数值精度结合尚不明确。

Method: 通过系统预实验发现：(1)LLM嵌入包含出价相关线索但无法替代数值特征；(2)收益仅在语义-数值精心集成时出现。基于此提出SemBid，在离线出价轨迹的Token级别注入三种语义输入（Task、History、Strategy），与数值轨迹Token一起通过自注意力机制整合。

Result: 在多场景和多预算设置下，SemBid在整体性能、约束满足和鲁棒性方面均优于离线RL和生成式序列建模的竞争基线。

Conclusion: 语义-数值的Token级融合是提升自动出价性能的有效路径，代码已开源。

Abstract: Auto-bidding is a crucial task in real-time advertising markets, where policies must optimize long-horizon value under delivery constraints (e.g., budget and CPA). Existing methods for auto-bidding rely on compact numerical state representations: while they can implicitly capture delivery dynamics, they offer limited support for explicitly representing and controlling high-level intent, evolving feedback, and operator-style strategic guidance in real campaigns. Meanwhile, Large Language Models (LLMs) offer a powerful method for encoding semantic information, it remains unclear when LLMs help and how to integrate them without sacrificing numerical precision. Through systematic preliminary studies, we find that (1) LLM embeddings contain bidding-relevant cues yet cannot replace numerical features, and (2) gains emerge only with careful semantic--numeric integration rather than naive concatenation. Motivated by these findings, we propose \textit{SemBid}, a novel auto-bidding framework that injects LLM-encoded semantics into offline bidding trajectories at the token level. SemBid introduces three semantic inputs: \textit{Task}, \textit{History}, and \textit{Strategy}. It injects these semantics as tokens alongside numerical trajectory tokens and uses self-attention to integrate them, improving controllability and generalization across objectives. Across diverse scenarios and budget regimes, SemBid outperforms competitive baselines from offline RL and generative sequence modeling, with more consistent gains in overall performance, constraint satisfaction, and robustness. Our code is available at: \href{https://github.com/AlanYu04/SemBid-KDD2026}{\textcolor{blue}{here}}.

</details>


### [205] [Taklif.AI: LLM-Powered Platform for Interest-Based Personalized College Assignments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05842)
*Zaki Kurdya,Mohammed Zuqlam,Salem Amassi,Shady Telbany,Motaz Saad*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Taklif.AI 是一个利用大语言模型（Llama 3.3 70B）根据学生课外兴趣和文化背景自动生成个性化作业的平台。初步测试（68人）显示84%参与者认为个性化功能有益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统"一刀切"式作业导致学生参与度下降和抄袭行为增加，亟需能兼顾学生多元兴趣和认知能力的个性化作业生成方案。

Method: 采用基于AWS的Serverless架构（Next.js），通过结构化提示工程（Prompt Engineering）结合输入/输出护栏，利用Llama 3.3 70B（经LiteLLM负载均衡和LangChain编排）生成个性化作业。

Result: 68名参与者（65学生+3教师）的用户验收测试中，84%认为个性化功能有益。

Conclusion: Taklif.AI展示了将学生课外兴趣和文化背景融入作业生成的可行性，但需进一步开展严格的学习效果实证评估。

Abstract: Educators face significant challenges in creating engaging, personalized assignments that accommodate students' diverse interests and cognitive abilities. Traditional one-size-fits-all assignments frequently lead to decreased student engagement and increased reliance on unethical practices such as plagiarism. To address these challenges, we present Taklif.AI, a platform that leverages Large Language Models (LLMs) to automatically generate personalized assignments tailored to individual student interests. Unlike existing AI-powered educational platforms that personalize based on academic performance metrics alone, Taklif.AI incorporates students' extracurricular interests and cultural contexts into the assignment generation process through a structured prompt engineering pipeline with input and output guardrails. The platform employs a serverless architecture on AWS with Next.js, using Llama 3.3 70B as the primary LLM via LiteLLM for multi-provider load balancing and LangChain for prompt orchestration. We describe the system architecture, the prompt design methodology, and the guardrails framework that ensures output quality. Preliminary user acceptance testing with 68 participants (65 students and 3 educators) indicates positive reception, with 84% of participants rating the personalization feature as beneficial. We discuss the platform's current capabilities and limitations, and outline directions for rigorous empirical evaluation of learning outcomes.

</details>


### [206] [AirQualityBench: A Realistic Evaluation Benchmark for Global Air Quality Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05854)
*Xing Xu,Xu Wang,Yudong Zhang,Huilin Zhao,Zhengyang Zhou,Yang Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了AirQualityBench——一个全球多污染物空气质量预测基准，包含3720个站点、6种污染物、真实缺失模式，揭示在预处理数据集上表现良好的模型在真实监测场景中性能会显著下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有空气质量预测模型通常在预处理过的干净数据集上评估（缺失值被移除或填充），这掩盖了真实监测网络中的关键挑战：全球覆盖不均衡、结构化缺失、污染物尺度异质性和部署成本。

Method: 构建包含2019-2025年全球3720个监测站点的原始观测数据，保留原生缺失模式，不进行数据补全；将缺失暴露为预测问题的一部分；在逆变换回物理浓度尺度后评估对未来有效观测的预测误差。

Result: 在预处理干净数据集上表现优异的时空模型，在全局、碎片化的真实监测数据上无法可靠迁移其性能。

Conclusion: AirQualityBench为可扩展、对缺失感知、物理可解释的空气质量预测提供了真实测试平台。

Abstract: Air-quality forecasting models are commonly evaluated on regional, preprocessed, and normalized datasets, where missing observations are removed or artificially completed. Such protocols simplify comparison but hide the conditions that dominate real monitoring networks: uneven global coverage, structured missingness, heterogeneous pollutant scales, and deployment cost. We introduce \textbf{AirQualityBench}, a global multi-pollutant benchmark designed to evaluate forecasting models under these realistic conditions. The benchmark contains hourly observations from 3,720 monitoring stations over 2021--2025, covers six major pollutants, and preserves provider-native observation masks. Rather than imputing a dense data tensor, AirQualityBench exposes missingness as part of the forecasting problem and reports errors on valid future observations after inverse transformation to physical concentration scales. Evaluating representative spatio-temporal models under this unified protocol shows that strong performance on sanitized datasets does not reliably transfer to global, fragmented monitoring streams. AirQualityBench therefore serves as a realistic testbed for scalable, mask-aware, and physically interpretable air-quality forecasting. All benchmark data, code, evaluation scripts, and baseline implementations are available at \href{https://github.com/Star-Learning/AirQualityBench}{GitHub}.

</details>


### [207] [SANEmerg: An Emergent Communication Framework for Semantic-aware Agentic AI Networking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05861)
*Yong Xiao,Haoran Zhou,Yujie Zhou,Marwan Krunz*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出SANEmerg框架，面向语义感知的智能体AI网络（AgentNet），通过带宽自适应重要性过滤和基于最小描述长度（MDL）原则的复杂度正则化器，使协作智能体在有限带宽和计算约束下自发生成高效通信协议，显著提升任务准确性并降低带宽与计算开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统网络范式将通信与计算刚性解耦，难以支撑大规模智能体AI网络（AgentNet）中异构AI智能体之间的实时协作，导致效率低下。

Method: 提出SANEmerg框架，包含：(1) 带宽自适应重要性过滤，动态优先传输高贡献消息维度；(2) 基于最小描述长度（MDL）原则的复杂度正则化器，促进计算有界的信令涌现。

Result: 通过AgentNet原型实验，SANEmerg在任务准确性上显著优于现有方案，同时大幅降低了带宽和计算开销。

Conclusion: SANEmerg有效证明了在带宽和计算受限条件下，智能体间可涌现出高效的通信与信令协议，为未来智能体AI原生网络提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Future networking systems are envisioned to become part of an agentic AI-native ecosystem in which a vast number of heterogeneous and specialized AI agents cooperate seamlessly to fulfill complex user requirements in real time. However, traditional networking paradigms are characterized by a rigid decoupling of communication and computation, which often leads to significant inefficiencies in large-scale agentic AI networking (AgentNet) systems. Emergent communication offers a novel solution by enabling autonomous agents that support task-specific signaling protocols for information exchange and collaborative coordination. In this paper, we consider a multi-agent emergent communication framework, tailored for semantic-aware AgentNet systems in which the user's semantic intent can be automatically detected, inferred, and linked to a set of sub-tasks to be assigned to a set of agents. We investigate how communication and signaling protocols can emerge among collaborative agents with computationally bounded intelligence under stringent bandwidth constraints. Our proposed framework, called SANEmerg, is designed to facilitate the emergence of communication for collaborative task fulfillment while adhering to the physical limits of AgentNet. SANEmerg incorporates a bandwidth-adaptable importance-filter that dynamically prioritizes the transmission of higher-contribution message dimensions, ensuring robust performance in bandwidth-limited environments. Furthermore, SANEmerg integrates a complexity-regularizer grounded in the Minimum Description Length (MDL) principle to facilitate the emergence of computationally bounded signaling. Evaluated via an AgentNet prototype and extensive experimentation, SANEmerg demonstrates significant performance improvements over state-of-the-art solutions, achieving superior task accuracy while significantly reducing bandwidth and computational overhead.

</details>


### [208] [XDecomposer: Learning Prior-Free Set Decomposition for Multiphase X-ray Diffraction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05866)
*Hanyu Gao,Bin Cao,Yunyue Su,Tong-Yi Zhang,Qiang Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出XDecomposer，一个无需先验知识的框架，用于多相XRD图谱的联合分解与物相识别，将多相衍射分析建模为集合预测问题，通过相位查询驱动分解机制实现高精度物相分离与识别。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决多相粉末X射线衍射（PXRD）分析中，真实合成样品常产生复杂混合物，现有单相方法无法有效分离多相成分这一核心瓶颈。

Method: 将多相衍射分析形式化为集合预测问题，设计相位查询驱动的分解机制，结合衍射一致的物理重建，在统一架构中推断无序的物相组成集合、混合比例及对应的结构表示。

Result: 在模拟和实验数据集上，XDecomposer在多种化学体系中显著提升了重建精度和物相识别性能，并展现出对未见混合物的强泛化能力。

Conclusion: 该方法为数据驱动的、源解析的多相XRD分析提供了实用路径，减少了对传统先验引导迭代相位匹配的长期依赖。

Abstract: Multiphase powder X-ray diffraction (PXRD) analysis remains a fundamental bottleneck in structure identification, as real-world synthesis often produces complex mixtures whose constituent phases (components) cannot be reliably disentangled. While recent advances in representation-based crystal retrieval and generation suggest the possibility of inferring structures directly from PXRD, existing approaches largely assume single-phase inputs and break down in multiphase settings. Here, we present XDecomposer, a prior-free framework for joint decomposition and identification of multiphase XRD patterns without requiring candidate phase lists, structural templates, or prior knowledge of phase number. We formulate multiphase diffraction analysis as a set prediction problem, where the model infers an unordered set of phase-resolved components, their mixture proportions, and corresponding structural representations within a unified architecture. A phase-query-driven decomposition mechanism, together with diffraction-consistent physical reconstruction, enables accurate source separation while preserving crystallographic fidelity. Extensive experiments on both simulated and experimental datasets show that XDecomposer substantially improves reconstruction accuracy and phase identification across diverse chemical systems, while maintaining strong generalization to unseen mixtures. These results provide a practical route toward data-driven, source-resolved multiphase XRD analysis and reduce long-standing dependence on prior-guided iteratively phase matching. The code is openly available at https://github.com/Licht0812/XDecomposer

</details>


### [209] [Agentic, Context-Aware Risk Intelligence in the Internet of Value](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05878)
*Basel Magableh,OmniRisk Research*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向价值互联网（IoV）的五引擎组合风险原语框架，涵盖价格/流动性预测、Bittensor去中心化验证、情感融合、智能体约束引擎及API风险引擎，并通过Solana上的27小时流动性压力测试和168小时预测路由校准实验验证其可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: IoV是一个异构、部分可信的网络，其主导边际风险是复合性的（路径、情绪、流动性、政策），而非单一链的属性。现有风险原语不足以应对这种复合风险，因此需要一种新的组合式风险原语。

Method: 提出五个引擎的组合架构：(1) 价格、流动性、波动率及路径健康的预测引擎；(2) Bittensor验证子网，去中心化并对预测输出进行经济评分；(3) 文本、链上流量及灰色文献的情感融合引擎；(4) 在宪法性、角色约束下的智能体引擎；(5) API风险与场景引擎，将预测转化为蒙特卡洛场景生成下的预承诺行动程序。实证锚定于两个实验：Solana上27小时政策约束的流动性压力测试，以及168小时预测路由校准弧线。

Result: 案例研究支持该框架的可部署性；验证器损失分解以形式化、可证伪的方式陈述。

Conclusion: 该组合风险原语适用于IoV的复合风险环境，具有形式化基础和实证支撑，为去中心化价值网络的风险管理提供了新范式。

Abstract: The Internet of Value (IoV) is a heterogeneous, partially-trusted network in which the dominant marginal risk is composite (route, sentiment, liquidity, and the policy a system is willing to commit to) rather than a property of any single chain. We argue that a risk primitive adequate for this regime is a composition of five engines: a prediction engine over price, liquidity, volatility, and route health; a Bittensor verification subnet that decentralises and economically scores prediction outputs; a sentiment-fusion engine over text, on-chain flow, and grey-literature feeds; an agentic engine under constitutional, role-bound action constraints; and an API-risk and scenario engine that converts forecasts into pre-committed action programs in the sense of Monte-Carlo scenario generation. We anchor the architecture in two empirical artefacts: a 27-hour policy-constrained liquidity stress-response experiment on Solana, and a 168-hour prediction-router calibration arc reported with explicit class-imbalance honesty. The case study supports deployability; the validator-loss decomposition is stated formally and is falsifiable.

</details>


### [210] [Null Space Constrained Contrastive Visual Forgetting for MLLM Unlearning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05909)
*Yuhang Wang,Zhenxing Niu,Haoxuan Ji,Guangyu He,Linlin Zhang,Haichang Gao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文针对多模态大语言模型(MLLM)的机器遗忘问题，提出通过冻结LLM主干、微调视觉模块的方法，结合对比视觉遗忘(CVF)机制和零空间约束，在遗忘目标视觉知识的同时保留非目标视觉知识和所有文本知识，并扩展至持续遗忘场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MLLM中视觉与文本模态紧密交织，导致在遗忘目标视觉知识时难以平衡知识移除与知识保留，现有方法在跨模态场景下效果不佳。

Method: 1) 冻结LLM主干，只微调视觉模块；2) 提出对比视觉遗忘(CVF)机制，将目标视觉知识表征引导至特征空间的适当区域；3) 识别保留知识的零空间，将遗忘过程约束在该空间内以减少知识保留的退化；4) 扩展至持续遗忘场景。

Result: 在多种基准测试上的大量实验表明，该方法在有效遗忘和稳健知识保留之间取得了良好平衡。

Conclusion: 通过视觉模块微调与零空间约束相结合的策略，能够有效解决MLLM中视觉知识遗忘的模态干扰问题，同时支持持续遗忘场景。

Abstract: The core challenge of machine unlearning is to strike a balance between target knowledge removal and non-target knowledge retention. In the context of Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs), this challenge becomes even more pronounced, as knowledge is further divided into visual and textual modalities that are tightly intertwined. In this paper, we introduce an MLLM unlearning approach that aims to forget target visual knowledge while preserving non-target visual knowledge and all textual knowledge. Specifically, we freeze the LLM backbone and achieve unlearning by fine-tuning the visual module. First, we propose a Contrastive Visual Forgetting (CVF) mechanism to separate target visual knowledge from retained visual knowledge, guiding the representations of target visual concepts toward appropriate regions in the feature space. Second, we identify the null space associated with retained knowledge and constrain the unlearning process within this space, thereby significantly mitigating degradation in knowledge retention. Third, beyond static unlearning scenarios, we extend our approach to continual unlearning, where forgetting requests arrive sequentially. Extensive experiments across diverse benchmarks demonstrate that our approach achieves a strong balance between effective forgetting and robust knowledge retention.

</details>


### [211] [PREFER: Personalized Review Summarization with Online Preference Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05911)
*Millend Roy,Agostino Capponi,Vineet Goyal*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个在线学习框架，通过用户对生成摘要的反馈，动态学习用户潜在偏好，从而为每位用户生成个性化的产品评论摘要。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有电商摘要系统生成通用静态摘要，忽视了不同用户关心不同产品特征、且偏好会随交互变化的问题。

Method: 提出在线学习框架，通过用户对生成摘要的直接反馈，迭代地优化对用户偏好的理解，从而生成个性化摘要。

Result: 在Amazon Reviews'23数据集上的模拟实验表明，在线偏好学习能够更好地对齐目标用户的兴趣，同时保持摘要质量。

Conclusion: 在线偏好学习能有效提升个性化摘要与用户兴趣的对齐度，是解决未知潜在偏好问题的可行方案。

Abstract: Product reviews significantly influence purchasing decisions on e-commerce platforms. However, the sheer volume of reviews can overwhelm users, obscuring the information most relevant to their specific needs. Current e-commerce summarization systems typically produce generic, static summaries that fail to account for the fact that (i) different users care about different product characteristics, and (ii) these preferences may evolve with interactions. To address the challenge of unknown latent preferences, we propose an online learning framework that generates personalized summaries for each user. Our system iteratively refines its understanding of user preferences by incorporating feedback directly from the generated summaries over time. We provide a case study using the Amazon Reviews'23 dataset, showing in controlled simulations that online preference learning improves alignment with target user interests while maintaining summary quality.

</details>


### [212] [Wisteria: A Unified Multi-Scale Feature Learning Framework for DNA Language Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05913)
*Weihua Wang,Haoji Li,Feilong Bao,Lei Yang,Guanglai Gao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Wisteria是一个结合局部基序捕获与全局依赖建模的DNA语言模型，通过门控膨胀卷积、门控MLP和傅里叶注意力机制实现多尺度基因组序列分析，在多个基准测试中表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有DNA语言模型主要关注长程token交互，忽视了局部基序与全局依赖之间的相互作用，无法有效捕捉基因组的多尺度特征。

Method: 在Mamba架构基础上增加门控膨胀卷积（捕获局部基序）和门控多层感知机（细化全局依赖），并引入基于傅里叶的注意力机制实现频域建模、周期扩展和长度泛化。

Result: 在四种包含短程和长程依赖的实验设置中，Wisteria在多个下游基准任务上超越了其他竞争性DNA语言模型基线。

Conclusion: Wisteria有效地统一了局部和全局依赖建模，实现了多尺度基因组序列分析，验证了融合多尺度特征学习框架的有效性。

Abstract: DNA language model aims to decipher the regulatory grammar and semantic of genomes by capturing long range dependencies in DNA sequences. Existing methods emphasize long range token interactions but often ignore the interplay between local motifs and global dependencies. In this paper, we propose Wisteria, a genomic language model that integrates multi scale feature learning within a unified framework for DNA sequence. Specifically, Wisteria augments the Mamba based architecture with gated dilated convolutions to capture local motifs and regulatory patterns, while gated multilayer perceptrons refine global dependencies. We further introduce a Fourier based attention mechanism to support frequency domain modeling, periodic extension and length generalization. Across four experimental settings with both short and long range dependencies, Wisteria demonstrates strong performance on downstream benchmarks against competitive DNA language model baselines. These results indicate that Wisteria effectively unifies local and global dependency modeling for multi scale genomic sequence analysis.

</details>


### [213] [Intentmaking and Sensemaking: Human Interaction with AI-Guided Mathematical Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05921)
*Alex Bäuerle,Adam Connors,Alexander Novikov,Adam Zsolt Wagner,Ngân Vũ,Fernanda Viegas,Martin Wattenberg,Lucas Dixon*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 通过11位数学专家使用进化编码代理AlphaEvolve的用户研究，发现并定义了"意图制造"（intentmaking）这一工作流——即通过主动与系统交互来迭代发现、定义和完善实验目标的过程，并将其视为意义建构（sensemaking）的自然延伸。研究表明用户会在意图制造和意义建构之间循环往复，为设计AI科学发现工具提供了新思路。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI为科学发现提供了强大工具，但如何有效利用这些系统的交互范式仍未得到充分探索。现有系统多采用简单的问答模式，缺乏对用户如何通过系统交互来定义和优化实验目标的理解。

Method: 对11位数学专家进行形成性用户研究，让他们使用进化编码代理AlphaEvolve解决专业领域中的高级问题，通过观察和分析发现用户的工作流模式。

Result: 识别并描述了"意图制造"这一独特工作流，将其定义为通过主动系统交互来迭代发现、定义和优化实验目标的过程。发现用户会在意图制造（定义和更新实验）与意义建构（解读结果）之间反复循环。

Conclusion: AI科学发现工具的设计应超越当前的黑箱问答模式，将AI视为协作工具而非不透明的助手，从而更好地支持用户的意图制造与意义建构循环。

Abstract: Artificial intelligence offers powerful new tools for scientific discovery, but the interaction paradigms required to effectively harness these systems remain underexplored. In this paper, we present findings from a formative user study with 11 expert mathematicians who used AlphaEvolve, an evolutionary coding agent, to tackle advanced problems in their fields of expertise. We identify and characterize a distinct workflow we term intentmaking, the iterative process of discovering, defining, and refining one's experimental goals through active system interaction. We frame this as a natural extension to sensemaking, the cognitive process of building an understanding of complex or novel data. We suggest that users enter a cycle of intentmaking (defining and updating their experiment) and sensemaking (interpreting the results) which repeats many times during the course of an investigation. Our documentation of these themes suggests an approach to designing AI tools for scientific discovery that goes beyond the existing question/answer model of many current systems, treating them as collaborative instruments rather than opaque black-box assistants.

</details>


### [214] [In Data or Invisible: Toward a Better Digital Representation of Low-Resource Languages with Knowledge Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05931)
*Ndeye-Emilie Mbengue*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该博士论文旨在解决数字技术加剧的高资源与低资源语言间开放关联数据（OAD）的鸿沟，通过分析DBpedia、BabelNet和Wikidata三大多语言知识图谱中的语言分布特征，研究跨语言迁移选择策略（基于语言邻近性和对齐标注）对知识图谱补全的影响，并探索利用类比推理来提升低资源语言覆盖度的方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 新兴数字技术加剧了开放关联数据中高资源语言与低资源语言之间的数字鸿沟，导致许多语言社区被排除在全球数字化转型之外，迫切需要改善多语言知识图谱的语言覆盖度。

Method: 1）识别影响语言分布的关键变量（如维基百科文章数量、语言标记实体数）；2）在DBpedia、BabelNet和Wikidata三大知识图谱中分析语言分布特征；3）研究跨语言迁移候选选择策略（基于语言邻近性和对齐标注）对多语言知识图谱补全的影响；4）探索基于类比推理（利用语言间的相似性/差异性）来识别跨语言对应关系的方法。

Result: 目前该文为博士开题报告，尚未报告具体实验结果，但提出了完整的研究框架和假设，包括语言分布的分析结果预期以及对跨语言迁移和类比推理策略有效性的验证计划。

Conclusion: 通过系统性分析语言分布特征并创新性地引入类比推理方法，有望有效改善低资源语言在关联开放数据中的覆盖度，促进数字普惠。

Abstract: Emerging digital technologies are exacerbating the existing divide in Open Access Data (OAD) between high-and low-resource languages, excluding many communities from participating in the global digital transformation. In this PhD proposal, we aim to address this gap, focusing on the language coverage of Linked Open Data knowledge graphs (LOD KGs). First, we identify key variables that characterize language distribution in LOD, including the number of Wikipedia articles per language edition and the number of language-tagged entities in LOD KGs. These variables are analyzed across three major multilingual LOD KGs, DBpedia, BabelNet, and Wikidata, providing insights into the representation and distribution of languages within LOD. Building on this analysis, we intend to study the impact of cross-lingual transfer candidate selection on the task of multilingual KG completion. In particular, we plan to investigate strategies based on linguistic proximity and the availability of curated annotated alignments between languages. Language proximity also motivates us to explore the benefits of analogical reasoning that relies on (dis)similarities and has not yet been investigated to identify correspondences across languages to improve KG completion performance and enhance language coverage in LOD.

</details>


### [215] [ICU-Bench:Benchmarking Continual Unlearning in Multimodal Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05938)
*Yuhang Wang,Wenjie Mei,Junkai Zhang,Guangyu He,Zhenxing Niu,Haichang Gao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ICU-Bench是一个面向持续多模态遗忘的基准，包含1000份隐私文档、9500张图像和100个遗忘任务，用于评估多模态大模型在持续隐私删除场景下的遗忘效果、历史遗忘保持、保留效用和稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准主要关注静态或短序列遗忘，无法有效评估实际部署中持续出现的隐私删除请求，亟需构建支持持续多模态遗忘评估的基准。

Method: 构建了包含医疗报告和劳动合同两个领域的1000份隐私敏感档案，共9500张图像、16000个问答对和100个遗忘任务，并引入新的持续遗忘评估指标。

Result: 现有遗忘方法在持续设置下普遍表现不佳，在遗忘质量、效用保持和长任务序列可扩展性方面存在明显局限。

Conclusion: 需要专门为持续隐私删除场景设计的多模态遗忘方法，当前方法无法满足实际部署中的持续遗忘需求。

Abstract: Although Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) have achieved remarkable progress across many domains, their training on large-scale multimodal datasets raises serious privacy concerns, making effective machine unlearning increasingly necessary. However, existing benchmarks mainly focus on static or short-sequence settings, offering limited support for evaluating continual privacy deletion requests in realistic deployments. To bridge this gap, we introduce ICU-Bench, a continual multimodal unlearning benchmark built on privacy-critical document data. ICU-Bench contains 1,000 privacy-sensitive profiles from two document domains, medical reports and labor contracts, with 9,500 images, 16,000 question-answer pairs, and 100 forget tasks. Additionally, new continual unlearning metrics are introduced, facilitating a comprehensive analysis of forgetting effectiveness, historical forgetting preservation, retained utility, and stability throughout the continual unlearning process. Through extensive experiments with representative unlearning methods on ICU-Bench, we show that existing methods generally struggle in continual settings and exhibit clear limitations in balancing forgetting quality, utility preservation, and scalability over long task sequences. These findings highlight the need for multimodal unlearning methods explicitly designed for continual privacy deletion.

</details>


### [216] [MAS-Algorithm: A Workflow for Solving Algorithmic Programming Problems with a Multi-Agent System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05949)
*Yuliang Xu,Xiang Xu,Yao Wan,Hu Wei,Tong Jia*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出MAS-Algorithm，一种用于算法问题求解的多智能体工作流框架，通过模块化分解求解过程、工具集成和智能体协调，在多个Qwen模型上实现了平均6.48%的接受率提升，远超参数高效微调的0.89%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的AI算法求解方法主要依赖模型中心策略（如架构修改、数据扩展），成本高且可解释性有限；而外部工具或提示技术（如思维链）则零散缺乏统一框架。因此，需要一个系统化、模块化、可扩展的多智能体工作流来提升结构化推理能力。

Method: 提出的MAS-Algorithm框架受竞争性程序员和算法工程师的实践启发，将端到端的求解过程分解为多个模块化阶段，每个阶段由专门的智能体负责，实现结构化推理、工具集成和智能体间的灵活协调。

Result: 在自建基准上，Qwen系列模型平均接受率提升6.48%（对比PEFT仅0.89%）；在LiveCodeBench-Pro上获得4.72%的提升；单个智能体贡献可达27.7%的改进。

Conclusion: MAS-Algorithm通过多智能体协作和模块化分解，显著提升了AI在算法推理任务上的表现，具备强可扩展性和可解释性，为AI驱动的算法推理提供了有前景的方向。

Abstract: Algorithmic problem solving serves as a rigorous testbed for evaluating structured reasoning in AI coding systems, as it directly reflects a model's ability to perform structured reasoning in complex scenarios.Existing approaches predominantly rely on model-centric strategies, such as architectural modifications and data scaling, which are costly and offer limited interpretability. Alternative methods leveraging external tools or prompting techniques (e.g., chain-of-thought) are often fragmented and lack a unified framework. In this paper, we propose MAS-Algorithm, a systematic multi-agent workflow for algorithmic problem solving inspired by the practices of competitive programmers and algorithm engineers. Our framework decomposes the end-to-end solving process into modular stages, enabling structured reasoning, tool integration, and flexible coordination among agents. The design emphasizes both rigor and extensibility, allowing it to generalize across diverse problem types.Experimental results on a self-constructed benchmark demonstrate consistent improvements across multiple Qwen series models, achieving an average gain of 6.48% in acceptance rate. In contrast, parameter-efficient fine-tuning on the same data yields only a marginal improvement of 0.89%. We further observe a 4.72% gain on LiveCodeBench-Pro, along with consistent improvements across additional accuracy and efficiency metrics.Beyond performance gains, we conduct comprehensive analyses to better understand the reasoning process within the workflow, including error patterns and cross-scenario behaviors. We further perform customized replacement and ablation studies to explore the upper bound of the framework, showing that individual agents can contribute improvements of up to 27.7%. These results highlight the strong potential of MAS-Algorithm for advancing AI-driven algorithmic reasoning.

</details>


### [217] [HaM-World: Soft-Hamiltonian World Models with Selective Memory for Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05951)
*Haoyun Tang,Haodong Cui,Keyao Xu,Kun Wang,Zhandong Mei*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: HaM-World (HMW) 提出了一种结构化世界模型，将潜状态分解为规范 (q, p) 子空间和上下文子空间 c，利用 Mamba 选择性状态空间记忆和哈密顿向量场实现长时域规划，在 DeepMind Control Suite 和 OOD 测试中显著优于基线。


<details>
